 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# M2b motion timing reference
     2	
     3	This is a single-screen lookup for the developer landing M2b. It
     4	re-tabulates the spec §Motion budget rows that M2b activates, names
     5	each surface, and pins the implementation idiom. Reduced-motion
     6	fallback behavior is documented per surface.
     7	
     8	## M2b's three new motion surfaces
     9	
    10	The chunk activates three motion surfaces from the spec's §Motion
    11	budget at `working/phase-5.md:84-95`. Two of them are signature
    12	details (#5 and #2 from §Design Language). **No other M2b surface
    13	animates.** The frozen motion budget permits animation only on
    14	`transform`, `opacity`, and `background-color` AND only on the
    15	specific surfaces listed in the spec table; M2b activates exactly
    16	the three rows below and nothing more.
    17	
    18	| #   | Surface                                  | Property            | Duration             | Easing                    | Trigger                            | Idiom                          |
    19	|-----|------------------------------------------|---------------------|----------------------|---------------------------|------------------------------------|--------------------------------|
    20	| 1   | **Tab indicator slide** *(signature #5)* | `transform: translateX` + `scaleX` | `--motion-base` (120 ms) | `--ease-standard` | active-tab change                  | CSS `transition: transform`    |
    21	| 2   | **Tab panel cross-fade-IN**              | `opacity: 0 → 1`    | `--motion-base` (120 ms) | `--ease-out`             | active-tab change (CSS `animation-name` toggle on `<div role="tabpanel">` from `none` → `tab-fade-in`) | CSS `@keyframes tab-fade-in` + animation-name re-trigger (NO React key remount) |
    22	| 3   | **Page-turn fade** *(signature #2)*      | `opacity: 0 → 1` + `transform: translateX(4px → 0)` | `--motion-disclosure` (200 ms) | `--ease-out`        | `selectedRowKey` change (mount of `<SessionView key={...}>`) | CSS `@keyframes session-page-turn` + React key remount on the OUTER session-pane element |
    23	
    24	## M2b's INSTANT (non-animated) surfaces
    25	
    26	These M2b surfaces undergo state changes with NO animation /
    27	transition. The change applies on the next paint without
    28	interpolation:
    29	
    30	| #   | Surface                                  | State change              | Trigger                  |
    31	|-----|------------------------------------------|---------------------------|--------------------------|
    32	| 4   | Tab text color (active ↔ inactive shift) | `--color-ink-muted` → `--color-ink` (bold) | active-tab change |
    33	| 5   | Tab text color (hover, inactive only)    | shifts via `color-mix`    | pointer enter            |
    34	| 6   | Copy path button hover                   | background tint           | pointer enter            |
    35	| 7   | Retry button hover                       | background tint           | pointer enter            |
    36	| 8   | "Open raw" anchor hover underline        | border-bottom color       | pointer enter            |
    37	| 9   | Tab focus-visible outline                | outline appears           | focus arrives            |
    38	
    39	Why instant rather than animated: `color`, `border-color`, and
    40	hover `background-color` on these surfaces are **NOT** in the
    41	spec's frozen motion budget at `working/phase-5.md:84-95`. The
    42	budget is load-bearing per spec line 1100; adding animations on
    43	unlisted surfaces requires a coordinator waiver, which M2b does not
    44	seek. Instant state changes still feel responsive at 60 Hz; the
    45	animated surfaces (#1, #2, #3) carry the chunk's animation budget.
    46	
    47	## Idiom details
    48	
    49	### Surface 1 — Tab indicator slide
    50	
    51	Single absolutely-positioned `<span class="indicator">` inside the
    52	tablist. Its `transform: translateX(...) scaleX(...)` is computed from
    53	the active tab's `getBoundingClientRect()` and applied imperatively
    54	on activation. The transition only animates `transform` (compositor-
    55	cheap; no layout reflow):
    56	
    57	```css
    58	.tabs .indicator {
    59	  position: absolute;
    60	  bottom: -1px;
    61	  left: 0;
    62	  width: 1px;                /* base extent — load-bearing, see below */
    63	  height: 1px;
    64	  background: var(--color-accent);
    65	  transform-origin: left;
    66	  transform: translateX(0) scaleX(0);
    67	  transition: transform var(--motion-base) var(--ease-standard);
    68	  pointer-events: none;
    69	}
    70	```
    71	
    72	The `width: 1px` is load-bearing: an empty absolutely-positioned
    73	`<span>` resolves to `width: auto` (= 0), and `scaleX(N) × 0 = 0`
    74	would render an invisible indicator. With the 1 px base, the JS-
    75	written `transform: translateX(${x}px) scaleX(${tabRect.width})`
    76	grows the indicator to the active tab's measured pixel width.
    77	
    78	On initial mount the indicator settles via `requestAnimationFrame`
    79	so font-swap metrics shifts (Fraunces fallback per M2a §4) don't
    80	strand the indicator off-target.
    81	
    82	### Surface 2 — Tab panel cross-fade-IN
    83	
    84	CSS animation-name re-trigger on the panel's own
    85	`<div role="tabpanel">` element. The panel's React subtree is
    86	**STABLE** across tab switches (Resolved Decision #12 / spec lines
    87	650–658 require visited panels to stay React-mounted so RawTab's
    88	`consumeRawPreview` consumer keeps streaming and so M3+
    89	`useParsedSession` results are not recomputed on every flip). The
    90	keyframe is defined once at module scope:
    91	
    92	```css
    93	@keyframes tab-fade-in {
    94	  from { opacity: 0; }
    95	  to   { opacity: 1; }
    96	}
    97	```
    98	
    99	The panel toggles its inline `animation` style based on `isActive`:
   100	
   101	```jsx
   102	<div role="tabpanel"
   103	     hidden={!isActive}
   104	     id={`panel-${tabId}`}
   105	     aria-labelledby={`tab-${tabId}`}
   106	     tabIndex={isActive && (tabId === "skim" || tabId === "transcript" || tabId === "raw") ? 0 : undefined}
   107	     style={{ animation: isActive
   108	                          ? "tab-fade-in 120ms var(--ease-out) both"
   109	                          : "none" }}>
   110	  {/* STABLE content — never remounted on tab change */}
   111	</div>
   112	```
   113	
   114	The browser fires the keyframe each time the inline `animation`
   115	property string changes from `"none"` to a real animation. Active →
   116	active re-renders DON'T re-fire (the property string is unchanged).
   117	`animation-fill-mode: both` ensures opacity stays at 1 after the
   118	animation completes. Outgoing panel hides instantly via the `hidden`
   119	attribute (display: none) — no fade-out.
   120	
   121	**Do NOT use a React `key={activeTab}` on the panel content** —
   122	that would unmount + remount the subtree on every tab change,
   123	aborting in-flight work and breaking the keep-mounted contract.
   124	Codex round 3 caught the previous artifact draft regressing to
   125	`key={activeTab}`; the corrected mechanism above does NOT touch the
   126	React tree on tab change.
   127	
   128	### Surface 3 — Page-turn fade
   129	
   130	CSS keyframes on `.session-pane`. React's `key={selectedRowKey}` on
   131	the outer `<article>` makes every selection change a fresh mount;
   132	the `@keyframes` fires automatically:
   133	
   134	```css
   135	.session-pane {
   136	  animation: session-page-turn var(--motion-disclosure) var(--ease-out) both;
   137	}
   138	
   139	@keyframes session-page-turn {
   140	  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateX(4px); }
   141	  to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateX(0); }
   142	}
   143	```
   144	
   145	The two surfaces (cross-fade + page-turn) fire on **separate DOM
   146	nodes** — one is the `.session-pane` root, the other is the active
   147	panel's own `<div role="tabpanel">` element (the same element that
   148	carries `hidden`, `tabIndex`, and the inline `animation` style toggle
   149	shown above; matches design.md §3.2 / §5.2 / §7.4 + prototype.html:567).
   150	They never collide. When a selection change also lands on a different
   151	default tab, both fire in parallel; the compositor handles them
   152	independently without re-layout.
   153	
   154	## Reduced-motion fallback
   155	
   156	The global rule in `apps/frontend/src/styles/global.css` (landed at
   157	M1a, unchanged in M2b):
   158	
   159	```css
   160	@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
   161	  *, *::before, *::after {
   162	    animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
   163	    animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
   164	    transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
   165	    scroll-behavior: auto !important;
   166	  }
   167	}
   168	```
   169	
   170	Effect on M2b's surfaces:
   171	
   172	- **Tab indicator slide**: zeroed transition → indicator teleports to
   173	  the active tab's coordinates without sliding.
   174	- **Tab cross-fade-IN**: zeroed animation duration → panel snaps to
   175	  opacity 1 immediately.
   176	- **Page-turn fade**: zeroed animation duration → new SessionView
   177	  appears at full opacity with no transform entrance.
   178	- **Instant hover + active surfaces (#4–#9)**: unaffected (they
   179	  weren't animated to begin with).
   180	
   181	The reading-wash noise overlay is also suppressed under reduced-motion
   182	(M2a established a SEPARATE rule for this since `background-image` is
   183	not in the global zero-out's property list). M2b doesn't change that.
   184	
   185	## Spec acceptance gates
   186	
   187	- §Motion table entries that M2b activates: row 1 (Tab strip
   188	  indicator), row 2 (Tab panel cross-fade), row 7 (Session-pane
   189	  content fade). All three are present in this artifact and
   190	  nothing else animates.
   191	- §Performance budget (lines 114–128): only `transform` + `opacity` +
   192	  `background-color` allowed AND only on the surfaces listed in the
   193	  spec table. M2b activates `transform` (#1, #3) and `opacity`
   194	  (#2, #3) and nothing else. M2b does NOT animate `color`,
   195	  `border-color`, or hover `background-color` on any surface.
   196	- §Reduced-motion (lines 97–112): all three M2b animations zero out
   197	  via the global rule. Verified at chunk close per checklist item
   198	  61 in `design.md`.
   199

   201	
   202	| Token                  | Value                                |
   203	|------------------------|--------------------------------------|
   204	| `--motion-fast`        | 80 ms                                |

   206	| `--motion-disclosure`  | 200 ms                               |
   207	| `--motion-pulse`       | 600 ms (M1a deep-link pulse — NOT M2b's surface, listed for reference) |
   208	| `--ease-standard`      | `cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)`       |
   209	| `--ease-out`           | `cubic-bezier(0.0, 0.0, 0.2, 1)`     |
   210	| `--ease-in-out`        | `cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.6, 1)`       |
   211

   214	from M2a.

     1	// Accessible tablist primitive for the Phase-5 right-pane tab strip.
     2	//
     3	// Implements the WAI-ARIA APG "tabs" pattern (automatic activation:
     4	// selection follows focus). Rendered as a flat `<div role="tablist">`
     5	// containing one `<button role="tab">` per tab plus a single
     6	// absolutely-positioned `<span class="indicator">` whose
     7	// `transform: translateX(...) scaleX(...)` is computed from the active
     8	// tab's bounding box and animated over `--motion-base` (120 ms)
     9	// `--ease-standard`.
    10	//
    11	// Controlled-only API: `value` + `onValueChange` come from the parent
    12	// (SessionView holds activeTab + visitedTabs). The primitive does NOT
    13	// own the active value, so M2b's `visitedTabs` mechanism stays in
    14	// SessionView and the panels themselves are rendered by the parent.
    15	//
    16	// Accessibility:
    17	//   - `<div role="tablist" aria-label>` wraps the four tab buttons.
    18	//   - Each `<button role="tab" id aria-controls aria-selected
    19	//     tabindex>` carries the WAI-ARIA APG roving-tabindex pattern:
    20	//     exactly one tab has `tabindex="0"` (the active one); the others
    21	//     have `tabindex="-1"`.
    22	//   - Selection follows focus: ArrowLeft / ArrowRight cycle (with
    23	//     wrap); Home / End jump to first / last; Enter / Space are
    24	//     no-ops because activation already happened on focus.
    25	//   - On click → activate + focus.
    26	//   - Indicator is `aria-hidden="true"` — purely decorative.
    27	//
    28	// Indicator measurement:
    29	//   - `useLayoutEffect` re-runs whenever `value` changes AND on
    30	//     `ResizeObserver` watching the tablist (covers viewport resize
    31	//     + Fraunces font swap remeasurement). Computes
    32	//     `tabRect.left - tablistRect.left` and `tabRect.width` directly
    33	//     via `getBoundingClientRect()` and writes a single
    34	//     `transform: translateX(${x}px) scaleX(${width}px)` declaration
    35	//     so the CSS `transition: transform` animates both components as
    36	//     one compositor-cheap step.
    37	//   - The indicator's CSS base size is `width: 1px; height: 1px;`
    38	//     (Tabs.css). `scaleX(N) × 1 px = N px`; without the 1 px base
    39	//     the empty span would resolve to `width: auto = 0` and the
    40	//     indicator would never appear.
    41	//   - Initial mount uses `requestAnimationFrame` to defer the first
    42	//     measurement by one frame so Fraunces' fallback metrics shifts
    43	//     (M2a §4 documented the ~1 px swap delta on Charter →
    44	//     Fraunces) don't strand the indicator off-target.
    45	import {
    46	  useCallback,
    47	  useEffect,
    48	  useLayoutEffect,
    49	  useRef,
    50	  type KeyboardEvent,
    51	  type ReactNode,
    52	} from "react";
    53	import "./Tabs.css";
    54	
    55	export type TabDescriptor<TabId extends string> = {
    56	  id: TabId;
    57	  label: string;
    58	  panel: ReactNode | (() => ReactNode);
    59	};
    60	
    61	export type TabsProps<TabId extends string> = {
    62	  /** ARIA tablist label; required for screen-reader context. */
    63	  ariaLabel: string;
    64	  /** Currently-active tab id (controlled). */
    65	  value: TabId;
    66	  /** Called when the active tab changes. */
    67	  onValueChange: (next: TabId) => void;
    68	  /** Tabs in render order. The label is the visible button text. */
    69	  tabs: ReadonlyArray<TabDescriptor<TabId>>;
    70	};
    71	
    72	export function Tabs<TabId extends string>({
    73	  ariaLabel,
    74	  value,
    75	  onValueChange,
    76	  tabs,
    77	}: TabsProps<TabId>) {
    78	  const tablistRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
    79	  const indicatorRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement | null>(null);
    80	  const tabButtonsRef = useRef<Map<TabId, HTMLButtonElement>>(new Map());
    81	
    82	  // Compute + apply the indicator transform. Returns true when the
    83	  // measurement landed (the active tab button was found in the
    84	  // refs map AND the tablist has rendered).
    85	  const measureIndicator = useCallback(() => {
    86	    const indicator = indicatorRef.current;
    87	    const tablist = tablistRef.current;
    88	    if (indicator === null || tablist === null) return false;
    89	    const activeButton = tabButtonsRef.current.get(value) ?? null;
    90	    if (activeButton === null) return false;
    91	    const tablistRect = tablist.getBoundingClientRect();
    92	    const tabRect = activeButton.getBoundingClientRect();
    93	    const x = tabRect.left - tablistRect.left;
    94	    const width = tabRect.width;
    95	    // One declaration so CSS `transition: transform` animates both
    96	    // components as one compositor-cheap step. The base CSS size is
    97	    // 1 × 1 px (see Tabs.css `.tabs .indicator`); `scaleX(N) × 1 = N`
    98	    // grows the indicator to the tab's measured pixel width.
    99	    indicator.style.transform = `translateX(${x}px) scaleX(${width}px)`;
   100	    return true;
   101	  }, [value]);
   102	
   103	  // Run measurement on every active-tab change. `useLayoutEffect`
   104	  // fires synchronously after DOM mutations but before paint, so the
   105	  // user never sees a single-frame "wrong indicator position".
   106	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   107	    measureIndicator();
   108	  }, [measureIndicator]);
   109	
   110	  // Initial mount: defer the FIRST measurement by one rAF so
   111	  // Fraunces fallback metrics settle before we read
   112	  // getBoundingClientRect (M2a §4 documented the ~1 px swap delta).
   113	  useEffect(() => {
   114	    const id = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   115	      measureIndicator();
   116	    });
   117	    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(id);
   118	    // mount-only; subsequent active-tab changes flow through the
   119	    // useLayoutEffect above.
   120	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   121	  }, []);
   122	
   123	  // ResizeObserver watches the tablist itself: viewport resize +
   124	  // Fraunces font swap both fire layout changes that re-flow the
   125	  // tab buttons. Re-measure on every observation.
   126	  useEffect(() => {
   127	    const tablist = tablistRef.current;
   128	    if (tablist === null) return;
   129	    if (typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined") return;
   130	    const observer = new ResizeObserver(() => {
   131	      measureIndicator();
   132	    });
   133	    observer.observe(tablist);
   134	    return () => observer.disconnect();
   135	  }, [measureIndicator]);
   136	
   137	  const activate = useCallback(
   138	    (id: TabId, focusButton: boolean) => {
   139	      onValueChange(id);
   140	      if (focusButton) {
   141	        const btn = tabButtonsRef.current.get(id);
   142	        if (btn !== undefined) btn.focus();
   143	      }
   144	    },
   145	    [onValueChange],
   146	  );
   147	
   148	  const onKeyDown = useCallback(
   149	    (event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
   150	      const key = event.key;
   151	      if (
   152	        key !== "ArrowLeft" &&
   153	        key !== "ArrowRight" &&
   154	        key !== "Home" &&
   155	        key !== "End"
   156	      ) {
   157	        return;
   158	      }
   159	      event.preventDefault();
   160	      const idx = tabs.findIndex((t) => t.id === value);
   161	      if (idx === -1) return;
   162	      let nextIdx: number;
   163	      if (key === "ArrowLeft") {
   164	        nextIdx = idx === 0 ? tabs.length - 1 : idx - 1;
   165	      } else if (key === "ArrowRight") {
   166	        nextIdx = idx === tabs.length - 1 ? 0 : idx + 1;
   167	      } else if (key === "Home") {
   168	        nextIdx = 0;
   169	      } else {
   170	        nextIdx = tabs.length - 1;
   171	      }
   172	      const nextTab = tabs[nextIdx];
   173	      if (nextTab !== undefined) {
   174	        activate(nextTab.id, true);
   175	      }
   176	    },
   177	    [tabs, value, activate],
   178	  );
   179	
   180	  return (
   181	    <div
   182	      ref={tablistRef}
   183	      role="tablist"
   184	      aria-label={ariaLabel}
   185	      className="tabs"
   186	      data-active-tab={value}
   187	      onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
   188	    >
   189	      {tabs.map((tab) => {
   190	        const isActive = tab.id === value;
   191	        return (
   192	          <button
   193	            key={tab.id}
   194	            ref={(el) => {
   195	              if (el === null) {
   196	                tabButtonsRef.current.delete(tab.id);
   197	              } else {
   198	                tabButtonsRef.current.set(tab.id, el);
   199	              }
   200	            }}
   201	            id={`tab-${tab.id}`}
   202	            type="button"
   203	            role="tab"
   204	            aria-controls={`panel-${tab.id}`}
   205	            aria-selected={isActive ? "true" : "false"}
   206	            tabIndex={isActive ? 0 : -1}
   207	            className="tab"
   208	            onClick={() => activate(tab.id, true)}
   209	          >
   210	            {tab.label}
   211	          </button>
   212	        );
   213	      })}
   214	      <span ref={indicatorRef} className="indicator" aria-hidden="true" />
   215	    </div>
   216	  );
   217	}

     1	/*

     3	 * surfaces 1 + 2.
     4	 *
     5	 * Selectors owned here:
     6	 *   - `.tabs`             → the `<div role="tablist">` shell. Hairline
     7	 *                           border-bottom + horizontal layout. NO
     8	 *                           background fill (inherits --color-surface
     9	 *                           from the session pane).
    10	 *   - `.tabs .tab`        → the `<button role="tab">` children. Text-
    11	 *                           only, no chrome. Active vs inactive is
    12	 *                           pure typographic (color + weight).
    13	 *   - `.tabs .indicator`  → the absolutely-positioned 1-px ink stroke
    14	 *                           that slides under the active tab. Base
    15	 *                           size is 1 × 1 px; the JS writes
    16	 *                           `transform: translateX(${x}px)
    17	 *                                       scaleX(${width}px)` to grow
    18	 *                           it to the active tab's measured width.
    19	 *
    20	 * Structural literals introduced here (per the design.md §9 invariant):
    21	 *   - `height: 2.5rem` on `.tabs` (40 px strip — spec line 935 +
    22	 *     design.md acceptance #16). WCAG 2.5.5 AAA touch-target shortfall
    23	 *     accepted per design.md §6.6.
    24	 *   - `padding-inline: 0` on `.tabs .tab` so the indicator slides
    25	 *     between LABEL widths (the measured `getBoundingClientRect().width`
    26	 *     equals the label width, not button-with-padding width — see
    27	 *     design.md acceptance #11).
    28	 *   - `bottom: -1px` on `.tabs .indicator` so the stroke sits on the
    29	 *     hairline border-bottom of `.tabs` rather than above it.
    30	 *

     1	// Right-pane shell for the Phase-5 split-pane master-detail layout.
     2	//
     3	// M2b rewires from the M1a four-state placeholder to a four-tab shell.
     4	// The state machine is preserved verbatim from M1a, with one
     5	// substitution: the M1a `ready-placeholder` state is REPLACED by
     6	// `ready` (the tab shell mounts inside it). Empty / loading /
     7	// session_not_found are unchanged from M1a.
     8	//
     9	// Tab shell (per design.md §3.2):
    10	//   - Minimal header: title (Fraunces italic at --text-xl) + tool
    11	//     badge + status pill + optional conflict badge. Copy path,
    12	//     subagent badge, sourcePathIsStale hint, and "Open raw" header
    13	//     anchor all defer to M4.
    14	//   - Tabs primitive (Transcript / Skim / Raw / Metadata) below.
    15	//     Default tab AT M2b = "metadata" (Resolved Decision #11; M4
    16	//     shifts to "transcript").
    17	//   - Lazy panel mounting (Resolved Decision #12 + spec lines
    18	//     650–658): a panel renders ONLY if `tabId === activeTab` OR
    19	//     `visitedTabs.has(tabId)`. Once activated, the panel stays
    20	//     React-mounted for the rest of the selection — preserves
    21	//     RawTab's in-flight stream + future TranscriptView /
    22	//     SkimView state.
    23	//   - Cross-fade-IN-only animation: the panel's own
    24	//     `<div role="tabpanel">` toggles its inline `style.animation`
    25	//     between "tab-fade-in 120ms var(--ease-out) both" (active) and
    26	//     "none" (inactive). The browser fires the keyframe each time
    27	//     the property string transitions from "none" to a real
    28	//     animation. The panel's React subtree is STABLE — never
    29	//     remounted on tab change.
    30	//   - Page-turn fade: the outer `<article class="session-pane">`
    31	//     carries `key={selectedRowKey}` so every selection change is a
    32	//     fresh React mount; the `@keyframes session-page-turn` rule in
    33	//     SessionView.css fires automatically on mount.
    34	//
    35	// tabIndex matrix (design.md §6.1 + acceptance item 29a — Option A):
    36	//   - Skim, Transcript, AND Raw active panels carry `tabIndex={0}`
    37	//     (none of them have an unconditional focusable child, so the
    38	//     panel itself must be the first Tab stop).
    39	//   - Metadata does NOT carry `tabIndex` — its Copy path button is
    40	//     always rendered and always focusable.
    41	//   - Inactive panels (`hidden=true`) MUST NOT carry `tabIndex`.
    42	//
    43	// Esc / "Back to list" / selection ownership are unchanged from
    44	// M1a (App.tsx still owns selectRow / narrowMode).
    45	import { useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
    46	import { Tabs } from "../../components/Tabs";
    47	import { SessionMetadata } from "./SessionMetadata";
    48	import { RawTab } from "./RawTab";
    49	import type { SessionRow } from "./types";
    50	import "./SessionView.css";
    51	
    52	export type SessionViewState =
    53	  | "empty"
    54	  | "loading"
    55	  | "ready"
    56	  | "session_not_found";
    57	
    58	export type TabId = "transcript" | "skim" | "raw" | "metadata";
    59	
    60	/**
    61	 * Default active tab when a selection arrives. M2b ships with
    62	 * Metadata; M4 will shift this to "transcript" alongside the
    63	 * TranscriptView landing (single-line edit + one test update).
    64	 */
    65	export const DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION: TabId = "metadata";
    66	
    67	export type SessionViewProps = {
    68	  state: SessionViewState;
    69	  /** Pinned-`now` ISO string used by the Metadata tab for relative-time labelling. Required when state === "ready". */
    70	  now?: string;
    71	  /** The merged row to render in the tab shell. Required when state === "ready". */
    72	  row?: SessionRow;
    73	  /** When true (narrow viewport + narrowMode === "session"), render the "← Back to list" affordance. */
    74	  showBackToList: boolean;
    75	  /** Called when the user clicks "← Back to list". Sets narrowMode = "list" only. */
    76	  onBackToList: () => void;
    77	  /** Called when the user clicks "Clear selection" in the session_not_found state. */
    78	  onClearSelection: () => void;
    79	  /** Called when the user clicks "Try Rescan" in the session_not_found state. */
    80	  onTryRescan: () => void;
    81	};
    82	
    83	export function SessionView(props: SessionViewProps) {
    84	  const {
    85	    state,
    86	    showBackToList,
    87	    onBackToList,
    88	    onClearSelection,
    89	    onTryRescan,
    90	  } = props;
    91	
    92	  return (
    93	    <article
    94	      className="session-pane"
    95	      data-state={state}
    96	      aria-busy={state === "loading"}
    97	      aria-live="polite"
    98	      aria-label="Session view"
    99	    >
   100	      {showBackToList ? (
   101	        <button
   102	          type="button"
   103	          className="back-to-list"
   104	          onClick={onBackToList}
   105	        >
   106	          ← Back to list
   107	        </button>
   108	      ) : null}
   109	      {state === "empty" ? (
   110	        <div className="session-state">
   111	          <EmptyPaneCopy />
   112	        </div>
   113	      ) : null}
   114	      {state === "loading" ? (
   115	        <div className="session-state">
   116	          <LoadingCopy />
   117	        </div>
   118	      ) : null}
   119	      {state === "session_not_found" ? (
   120	        <div className="session-state">
   121	          <SessionNotFoundCopy
   122	            onClearSelection={onClearSelection}
   123	            onTryRescan={onTryRescan}
   124	          />
   125	        </div>
   126	      ) : null}
   127	      {state === "ready" && props.row !== undefined && props.now !== undefined ? (
   128	        <ReadyTabShell row={props.row} now={props.now} />
   129	      ) : null}
   130	    </article>
   131	  );
   132	}
   133	
   134	function EmptyPaneCopy() {
   135	  return (
   136	    <>
   137	      <span className="empty-mark" aria-hidden="true">
   138	        ·
   139	      </span>
   140	      <p className="empty-prose-1">
   141	        Select a session from the list to view its content.
   142	      </p>
   143	      <p className="empty-prose-2">
   144	        The session view shows the full Transcript chronologically, a
   145	        Skim outline (one block per user message), the Raw NDJSON for
   146	        verification, and the session's Metadata.
   147	      </p>
   148	    </>
   149	  );
   150	}
   151	
   152	function LoadingCopy() {
   153	  return <p className="loading-line">Reading session…</p>;
   154	}
   155	
   156	function SessionNotFoundCopy({
   157	  onClearSelection,
   158	  onTryRescan,
   159	}: {
   160	  onClearSelection: () => void;
   161	  onTryRescan: () => void;
   162	}) {
   163	  return (
   164	    <>
   165	      <h2 className="not-found-heading">Session not found in current view</h2>
   166	      <p className="not-found-hint">
   167	        The session referenced by the URL was not in the merged set
   168	        after the latest scan.
   169	      </p>
   170	      <div className="not-found-actions">
   171	        <button

   173	          className="quiet-button"
   174	          onClick={onClearSelection}
   175	        >
   176	          Clear selection
   177	        </button>
   178	        <button
   179	          type="button"
   180	          className="quiet-button"
   181	          onClick={onTryRescan}
   182	        >
   183	          Try Rescan
   184	        </button>
   185	      </div>
   186	    </>
   187	  );
   188	}
   189	
   190	/**
   191	 * The four-tab shell that renders inside `state === "ready"`.
   192	 *
   193	 * Owns:
   194	 *   - `activeTab` state (controlled value passed to <Tabs>).
   195	 *   - `visitedTabs` Set (lazy-mount discipline).
   196	 *   - The minimal header (title / tool badge / status pill / optional
   197	 *     conflict badge).
   198	 *   - The four `<TabPanel>` wrappers with the cross-fade-IN-only
   199	 *     animation toggle.
   200	 *
   201	 * Reset on selection change is automatic — the parent
   202	 * `<SessionView key={selectedRowKey}>` (set in App.tsx) remounts
   203	 * this whole subtree, so `useState` re-initializes activeTab to
   204	 * `DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION` and visitedTabs to
   205	 * `new Set([DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION])`.
   206	 */
   207	function ReadyTabShell({
   208	  row,
   209	  now,
   210	}: {
   211	  row: SessionRow;
   212	  now: string;
   213	}) {
   214	  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabId>(DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION);
   215	  const [visitedTabs, setVisitedTabs] = useState<Set<TabId>>(
   216	    () => new Set<TabId>([DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION]),
   217	  );
   218	
   219	  // Defensive reset: if `row.rowKey` changes WITHOUT a parent-level
   220	  // remount (shouldn't happen given the App.tsx `key={selectedRowKey}`
   221	  // contract, but kept as a safety belt), snap activeTab back to the
   222	  // default and reset visitedTabs.
   223	  useEffect(() => {
   224	    setActiveTab(DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION);
   225	    setVisitedTabs(new Set<TabId>([DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION]));
   226	  }, [row.rowKey]);
   227	
   228	  const handleActivate = (next: TabId) => {
   229	    setActiveTab(next);
   230	    setVisitedTabs((prev) => {
   231	      if (prev.has(next)) return prev;
   232	      const nextSet = new Set(prev);
   233	      nextSet.add(next);
   234	      return nextSet;
   235	    });
   236	  };
   237	
   238	  // Object-keyed panel content — NEVER index-keyed (so an unvisited
   239	  // mid-sequence panel never causes React to re-key remaining panels
   240	  // by position). The OUTER `key={id}` on `<TabPanel>` is required;
   241	  // the INNER content tree must NOT carry any per-activation key
   242	  // (acceptance #27).
   243	  const panelContent: Record<TabId, ReactNode> = {
   244	    transcript: <TranscriptPlaceholder />,
   245	    skim: <SkimPlaceholder />,
   246	    raw: <RawTab row={row} />,
   247	    metadata: <SessionMetadata row={row} now={now} />,
   248	  };
   249	
   250	  const tabs = [
   251	    { id: "transcript" as const, label: "Transcript", panel: null },
   252	    { id: "skim" as const, label: "Skim", panel: null },
   253	    { id: "raw" as const, label: "Raw", panel: null },
   254	    { id: "metadata" as const, label: "Metadata", panel: null },
   255	  ];
   256	
   257	  return (
   258	    <>
   259	      <SessionPaneHeader row={row} />
   260	      <Tabs<TabId>
   261	        ariaLabel="Session content tabs"
   262	        value={activeTab}
   263	        onValueChange={handleActivate}
   264	        tabs={tabs}
   265	      />
   266	      <div className="session-pane-tabs">
   267	        {(Object.entries(panelContent) as Array<[TabId, ReactNode]>)
   268	          .filter(([id]) => visitedTabs.has(id))
   269	          .map(([id, content]) => (
   270	            <TabPanel key={id} id={id} isActive={id === activeTab}>
   271	              {content}
   272	            </TabPanel>
   273	          ))}
   274	      </div>
   275	    </>
   276	  );
   277	}
   278	
   279	function SessionPaneHeader({ row }: { row: SessionRow }) {
   280	  return (
   281	    <header className="session-pane-header">
   282	      <h2 className="session-title">{row.title ?? "(untitled)"}</h2>
   283	      <span className="session-tool-badge">{row.tool}</span>
   284	      <span className={`badge ${row.status.replace(/_/g, "-")}`}>
   285	        {row.status.replace(/_/g, " ")}
   286	      </span>
   287	      {row.statusConflict ? (
   288	        <span
   289	          className="badge session-conflict-badge"
   290	          title="Source and stored status disagreed during load — refresh to re-fetch."
   291	        >
   292	          Conflict
   293	        </span>
   294	      ) : null}
   295	    </header>
   296	  );
   297	}
   298	
   299	/**
   300	 * One tab panel. Renders its `<div role="tabpanel">` always; the
   301	 * `hidden` HTML attribute toggles visibility. The panel's React
   302	 * subtree is STABLE — never remounted on tab change (Resolved
   303	 * Decision #12). The cross-fade-IN-only animation is driven by the
   304	 * inline `style.animation` toggle.
   305	 *
   306	 * tabIndex matrix (design.md §6.1 acceptance item 29a):
   307	 *   - Skim, Transcript, Raw → tabIndex=0 when active.
   308	 *   - Metadata              → no tabIndex (Copy path button is always
   309	 *                             focusable).
   310	 *   - Inactive (hidden)     → no tabIndex.
   311	 */
   312	function TabPanel({
   313	  id,
   314	  isActive,
   315	  children,
   316	}: {
   317	  id: TabId;
   318	  isActive: boolean;
   319	  children: ReactNode;
   320	}) {
   321	  const wantsTabIndex =
   322	    isActive && (id === "skim" || id === "transcript" || id === "raw");
   323	  return (
   324	    <div
   325	      role="tabpanel"
   326	      id={`panel-${id}`}
   327	      aria-labelledby={`tab-${id}`}
   328	      hidden={!isActive}
   329	      tabIndex={wantsTabIndex ? 0 : undefined}
   330	      style={{
   331	        animation: isActive
   332	          ? "tab-fade-in 120ms var(--ease-out) both"
   333	          : "none",
   334	      }}
   335	    >
   336	      {children}
   337	    </div>
   338	  );
   339	}

     1	// Metadata tab body for the Phase-5 right-pane Tabs shell.
     2	//
     3	// Extracted from `SessionDetail.tsx` lines 130–256 (Phase 4 drawer
     4	// body) byte-equivalently per design.md §3.3:
     5	//   - Same 18 fields rendered as a `<dl class="metadata-meta">`
     6	//     with snake_case `<dt>` text and `mono`-classed `<dd>` for
     7	//     identifier-style values. The single exception is the
     8	//     source-path `<dt>`, which derives from `sourcePathLabel`
     9	//     ("Source path:" or "Last seen source path:") with the trailing
    10	//     colon stripped — matches Phase 4 verbatim.
    11	//   - Same Copy path button + Clipboard-API → manual-selection
    12	//     fallback. Hint copy preserved verbatim ("Copied" / "Selected
    13	//     — press Ctrl/Cmd + C to copy").
    14	//   - Same status pill recipe (the `.badge {variant}` pill is
    15	//     rendered inline; `variant = status.replace(/_/g, "-")`
    16	//     and the visible text is `status.replace(/_/g, " ")`).
    17	//   - Same "View raw" anchor recipe — rendered ONLY when
    18	//     `row.storedSessionUid !== null` (Phase 4 invariant preserved).
    19	//
    20	// NEW for M2b (Resolved Decision #8):
    21	//   - Subagent sidecar badge: dashed-hairline informational chip
    22	//     INLINE next to the `has_subagent_sidecars: true` value (NOT a
    23	//     top-of-pane banner). Reads as a footnote on the field rather
    24	//     than a warning at the top.
    25	//
    26	// SessionMetadata is presentational. The parent (SessionView, via
    27	// the Tabs primitive) passes `row` + `now`; this component owns no
    28	// fetch state and never reaches outside its props. The Phase-4
    29	// `<SessionDetail>` component remains on disk through M5 (Resolved
    30	// Decision #6) and is no longer reachable from the running app —
    31	// this extraction is a NEW component, NOT an in-place edit.
    32	import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
    33	import { relativeTimeFrom } from "./relativeTime";
    34	import type { SessionRow } from "./types";
    35	import "./SessionMetadata.css";
    36	
    37	export type SessionMetadataProps = {
    38	  row: SessionRow;
    39	  /** Pinned-`now` ISO string used for relative-time labelling. */
    40	  now: string;
    41	};
    42	
    43	const COPY_HINT_TIMEOUT_MS = 2000;
    44	
    45	export function SessionMetadata({ row, now }: SessionMetadataProps) {
    46	  const sourcePath = row.sourcePath;
    47	  const sourcePathRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement | null>(null);
    48	  const [copyHint, setCopyHint] = useState<
    49	    "idle" | "copied" | "fallback"
    50	  >("idle");
    51	
    52	  useEffect(() => {
    53	    if (copyHint === "idle") return;
    54	    const id = setTimeout(() => setCopyHint("idle"), COPY_HINT_TIMEOUT_MS);
    55	    return () => clearTimeout(id);
    56	  }, [copyHint]);
    57	
    58	  useEffect(() => {
    59	    setCopyHint("idle");
    60	  }, [row.rowKey]);
    61	
    62	  const handleCopyPath = async () => {
    63	    const clipboard = (
    64	      globalThis as unknown as {
    65	        navigator?: { clipboard?: { writeText?: (s: string) => Promise<void> } };
    66	      }
    67	    ).navigator?.clipboard;
    68	    if (clipboard?.writeText) {
    69	      try {
    70	        await clipboard.writeText(sourcePath);
    71	        setCopyHint("copied");
    72	        return;
    73	      } catch {
    74	        // Fall through to manual-select fallback.
    75	      }
    76	    }
    77	    const span = sourcePathRef.current;
    78	    if (span !== null) {
    79	      const range = document.createRange();
    80	      range.selectNodeContents(span);
    81	      const selection = (
    82	        globalThis as unknown as { getSelection?: () => Selection | null }
    83	      ).getSelection?.();
    84	      if (selection) {
    85	        selection.removeAllRanges();
    86	        selection.addRange(range);
    87	      }
    88	    }
    89	    setCopyHint("fallback");
    90	  };
    91	
    92	  const sourcePathLabel = row.sourcePathIsStale
    93	    ? "Last seen source path:"
    94	    : "Source path:";
    95	
    96	  const rawHref =
    97	    row.storedSessionUid !== null
    98	      ? `/api/v1/sessions/${row.storedSessionUid}/raw`
    99	      : null;
   100	
   101	  return (
   102	    <div className="session-metadata">
   103	      <dl className="metadata-meta">
   104	        <dt>session_key</dt>
   105	        <dd className="mono">{row.sourceSessionKey ?? "—"}</dd>
   106	
   107	        <dt>session_uid</dt>
   108	        <dd className="mono">{row.storedSessionUid ?? "—"}</dd>
   109	
   110	        <dt>row_key</dt>
   111	        <dd className="mono">{row.rowKey}</dd>
   112	
   113	        <dt>tool</dt>
   114	        <dd className="mono">{row.tool}</dd>
   115	
   116	        <dt>source_session_id</dt>
   117	        <dd className="mono">{row.sourceSessionId}</dd>
   118	
   119	        <dt>presence</dt>
   120	        <dd className="mono">{row.presence}</dd>
   121	
   122	        <dt>status</dt>
   123	        <dd>
   124	          <span className={`badge ${row.status.replace(/_/g, "-")}`}>
   125	            {row.status.replace(/_/g, " ")}
   126	          </span>
   127	          {row.statusConflict ? (
   128	            <>
   129	              {" "}
   130	              <span className="muted">(disagreed during load)</span>
   131	            </>
   132	          ) : null}
   133	        </dd>
   134	
   135	        <dt>status_conflict</dt>
   136	        <dd className="mono">{row.statusConflict ? "true" : "false"}</dd>
   137	
   138	        <dt>title</dt>
   139	        <dd>{row.title ?? <span className="muted">(untitled)</span>}</dd>
   140	
   141	        <dt>project_path</dt>
   142	        <dd className="mono">{row.projectPath ?? "—"}</dd>
   143	
   144	        <dt>{sourcePathLabel.replace(":", "")}</dt>
   145	        <dd>
   146	          <span className="mono metadata-source-path" ref={sourcePathRef}>
   147	            {sourcePath}
   148	          </span>
   149	          {" "}
   150	          <button
   151	            type="button"
   152	            className="metadata-copy-btn"
   153	            onClick={() => {
   154	              void handleCopyPath();
   155	            }}
   156	          >
   157	            Copy path
   158	          </button>
   159	          {copyHint === "copied" ? (
   160	            <>
   161	              {" "}
   162	              <span className="muted metadata-copy-hint">Copied</span>
   163	            </>
   164	          ) : null}
   165	          {copyHint === "fallback" ? (
   166	            <>
   167	              {" "}
   168	              <span className="muted metadata-copy-hint">
   169	                Selected — press Ctrl/Cmd + C to copy
   170	              </span>
   171	            </>
   172	          ) : null}
   173	        </dd>
   174	
   175	        <dt>source_path_is_stale</dt>
   176	        <dd className="mono">
   177	          {row.sourcePathIsStale ? "true" : "false"}
   178	        </dd>
   179	
   180	        <dt>source_fingerprint</dt>
   181	        <dd className="mono">{row.sourceFingerprint}</dd>
   182	
   183	        <dt>has_subagent_sidecars</dt>
   184	        <dd>
   185	          <span className="mono">
   186	            {row.hasSubagentSidecars ? "true" : "false"}
   187	          </span>
   188	          {row.hasSubagentSidecars ? (
   189	            <>
   190	              {" "}
   191	              <span
   192	                className="metadata-subagent-badge"
   193	                title="Has Claude Code subagent sidecars on disk — not ingested in v1"
   194	              >
   195	                Has Claude Code subagent sidecars on disk — not ingested in v1
   196	              </span>
   197	            </>
   198	          ) : null}
   199	        </dd>
   200	
   201	        <dt>stored_raw_ref</dt>
   202	        <dd className="mono">{row.storedRawRef ?? "—"}</dd>
   203	
   204	        <dt>created_at (source clock)</dt>
   205	        <dd>{renderTimestamp(now, row.createdAt)}</dd>
   206	
   207	        <dt>source_updated_at (source clock)</dt>
   208	        <dd>{renderTimestamp(now, row.sourceUpdatedAt)}</dd>
   209	
   210	        <dt>ingested_at (backend clock)</dt>
   211	        <dd>{renderTimestamp(now, row.ingestedAt)}</dd>
   212	      </dl>
   213	
   214	      {rawHref !== null ? (
   215	        <p className="metadata-raw-link-row">
   216	          <a
   217	            className="raw-link"
   218	            href={rawHref}
   219	            target="_blank"
   220	            rel="noopener noreferrer"
   221	          >
   222	            View raw
   223	          </a>
   224	        </p>
   225	      ) : null}
   226	    </div>
   227	  );
   228	}
   229	
   230	function renderTimestamp(now: string, value: string | null) {
   231	  if (value === null) {
   232	    return <span className="muted">—</span>;
   233	  }
   234	  return (
   235	    <>
   236	      <span className="mono">{value}</span>
   237	      {" "}
   238	      <span className="muted">({relativeTimeFrom(now, value)})</span>
   239	    </>
   240	  );

     1	// Raw tab body for the Phase-5 right-pane Tabs shell.
     2	//
     3	// Extracted from `SessionDetail.tsx`'s `RawPreviewBlock` byte-
     4	// equivalently (Phase 4 contract preserved). PLUS the M2b
     5	// "Not yet imported" branch when `row.storedSessionUid === null`
     6	// (Resolved Decision #18 — Metadata + Raw never trigger the
     7	// `useParsedSession` fetch; for source-only rows Raw cannot
     8	// reach `/api/v1/sessions/<uid>/raw` because the URL doesn't exist).
     9	//
    10	// State machine (RawPreviewState from `rawPreview.ts`):
    11	//   idle / loading → "Loading raw preview…" muted prose
    12	//   success        → <pre> of decoded NDJSON lines + caption
    13	//   error          → "Failed to load raw preview: <message>" + Retry
    14	//   non_2xx        → "HTTP <status>: <bodySnippet>" + Retry
    15	//   not_imported   → quiet muted prose (NEW for M2b; never fires
    16	//                    the fetch)
    17	//
    18	// AbortController cleanup (design.md §9 acceptance #40):
    19	//   The `useEffect` cleanup aborts the in-flight fetch ONLY on
    20	//     (1) selectedRowKey change → outer SessionView remounts →
    21	//         RawTab unmounts → cleanup runs;
    22	//     (2) row.storedSessionUid change → useEffect dep change;
    23	//     (3) Retry button bumps `attempt` → useEffect dep change;
    24	//     (4) SessionView itself unmounts.
    25	//   Tab switches are NOT a cleanup trigger — the panel stays
    26	//   React-mounted across tab switches per the keep-mounted contract
    27	//   (Resolved Decision #12 + spec lines 650–658). Switching tabs
    28	//   only toggles `hidden`, the inline `style.animation`, and
    29	//   `tabIndex` on the parent `<div role="tabpanel">` — RawTab's
    30	//   `useEffect` does NOT re-run.
    31	//
    32	// Caption strings preserved EXACTLY from Phase 4
    33	// (`describeCaption(lineCount, reachedLineCap, reachedByteCap)`):
    34	//   - byte cap fired → "Stopped at byte cap — full payload not
    35	//                       downloaded."
    36	//   - line cap fired → `Showing first ${N} lines of the raw payload.`
    37	//   - neither cap     → `Showing first ${N} lines (full payload below
    38	//                       the caps).`
    39	//   - both caps       → byte-cap caption wins.
    40	//
    41	// Non-JSON fallback line marker preserved verbatim ("(non-JSON
    42	// line)"); styling uses the muted-italic cascade in RawTab.css
    43	// (NOT the warn-color text — codex measured warn-on-surface-raised
    44	// at 3.97:1 light, which fails AA for normal text; muted passes —
    45	// see colors.md row R03).
    46	import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
    47	import { ApiError, streamSessionRaw } from "../../lib/api";
    48	import {
    49	  consumeRawPreview,
    50	  type RawPreviewLine,
    51	  type RawPreviewState,
    52	} from "./rawPreview";
    53	import type { SessionRow } from "./types";
    54	import "./RawTab.css";
    55	
    56	export type RawTabProps = {
    57	  row: SessionRow;
    58	};
    59	
    60	export function RawTab({ row }: RawTabProps) {
    61	  // Source-only rows short-circuit before any fetch state — render
    62	  // the "Not yet imported" branch.
    63	  if (row.storedSessionUid === null) {
    64	    return (
    65	      <div className="raw-tab">
    66	        <p className="raw-not-imported">
    67	          This session has not been imported yet — only the source-
    68	          side metadata is available. Click Import in the action bar
    69	          to fetch the raw payload.
    70	        </p>
    71	      </div>
    72	    );
    73	  }
    74	  return <RawTabFetching sessionUid={row.storedSessionUid} />;
    75	}
    76	
    77	function RawTabFetching({ sessionUid }: { sessionUid: string }) {
    78	  const [state, setState] = useState<RawPreviewState>({ kind: "loading" });
    79	  // Bumping `attempt` re-runs the effect. Same pattern as the Phase 4
    80	  // `RawPreviewBlock`.
    81	  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0);
    82	
    83	  useEffect(() => {
    84	    let cancelled = false;
    85	    const controller = new AbortController();
    86	    setState({ kind: "loading" });
    87	
    88	    void (async () => {
    89	      try {
    90	        const response = await streamSessionRaw(sessionUid, controller.signal);
    91	        const result = await consumeRawPreview(response, controller.signal);
    92	        if (!cancelled) {
    93	          setState(result);
    94	        }
    95	      } catch (err) {
    96	        if (isAbortError(err)) return;
    97	        if (cancelled) return;
    98	        if (err instanceof ApiError) {
    99	          setState({
   100	            kind: "non_2xx",
   101	            status: err.status,
   102	            bodySnippet: truncateForDisplay(err.body),
   103	          });
   104	          return;
   105	        }
   106	        const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
   107	        setState({ kind: "error", message });
   108	      }
   109	    })();
   110	
   111	    return () => {
   112	      cancelled = true;
   113	      controller.abort();
   114	    };
   115	  }, [sessionUid, attempt]);
   116	
   117	  const handleRetry = useCallback(() => {
   118	    setAttempt((a) => a + 1);
   119	  }, []);
   120	
   121	  if (state.kind === "idle" || state.kind === "loading") {
   122	    return (
   123	      <div className="raw-tab">
   124	        <p className="raw-loading">Loading raw preview…</p>
   125	      </div>
   126	    );
   127	  }
   128	
   129	  if (state.kind === "error") {
   130	    return (
   131	      <div className="raw-tab">
   132	        <p className="raw-error">
   133	          Failed to load raw preview: {state.message}
   134	        </p>
   135	        <p>
   136	          <button type="button" className="raw-retry" onClick={handleRetry}>
   137	            Retry
   138	          </button>
   139	        </p>
   140	      </div>
   141	    );
   142	  }
   143	
   144	  if (state.kind === "non_2xx") {
   145	    return (
   146	      <div className="raw-tab">
   147	        <p className="raw-error">
   148	          HTTP {state.status}: {state.bodySnippet}
   149	        </p>
   150	        <p>
   151	          <button type="button" className="raw-retry" onClick={handleRetry}>
   152	            Retry
   153	          </button>
   154	        </p>
   155	      </div>
   156	    );
   157	  }
   158	
   159	  // success
   160	  const { lines, reachedLineCap, reachedByteCap } = state;
   161	  const caption = describeCaption(
   162	    lines.length,
   163	    reachedLineCap,
   164	    reachedByteCap,
   165	  );
   166	
   167	  return (
   168	    <div className="raw-tab">
   169	      <pre className="raw-pre" aria-label="Raw NDJSON preview">
   170	        {lines.map((line, idx) => (
   171	          <RawTabLineRow key={idx} line={line} />
   172	        ))}
   173	      </pre>
   174	      <p className="raw-caption">{caption}</p>
   175	    </div>
   176	  );
   177	}
   178	
   179	function RawTabLineRow({ line }: { line: RawPreviewLine }) {
   180	  if (line.kind === "json") {
   181	    return <div className="line">{line.raw}</div>;
   182	  }
   183	  return (
   184	    <div className="line text">
   185	      {line.raw}
   186	      {" "}
   187	      <span className="raw-fallback-marker">(non-JSON line)</span>
   188	    </div>
   189	  );
   190	}
   191	
   192	function describeCaption(
   193	  lineCount: number,
   194	  reachedLineCap: boolean,
   195	  reachedByteCap: boolean,
   196	): string {
   197	  if (reachedByteCap) {
   198	    return "Stopped at byte cap — full payload not downloaded.";
   199	  }
   200	  if (reachedLineCap) {
   201	    return `Showing first ${lineCount} lines of the raw payload.`;
   202	  }
   203	  return `Showing first ${lineCount} lines (full payload below the caps).`;
   204	}
   205	
   206	function truncateForDisplay(body: string, maxChars = 240): string {
   207	  if (body.length <= maxChars) return body;
   208	  return `${body.slice(0, maxChars)}…`;
   209	}
   210	
   211	function isAbortError(err: unknown): boolean {
   212	  if (err === null || err === undefined) return false;
   213	  if (typeof err !== "object") return false;
   214	  const name = (err as { name?: unknown }).name;
   215	  return name === "AbortError";
   216	}

     1	// Inspection page.
     2	//
     3	// Orchestrates three parallel fetches — source sessions, stored sessions,
     4	// scan errors — via `Promise.allSettled` so each panel settles
     5	// independently. A failure on one panel does not blank the others.
     6	// Each panel owns its own `{ loading, ok, error }` slice of state.
     7	//
     8	// As of Phase 4 Milestone 5 (Chunk F):
     9	//   - The list is PAGINATED client-side (page sizes 50 / 100 / 200,
    10	//     default 50; persisted via `useSessionFilters`). `pageIndex` is
    11	//     a separate piece of view state owned here, NOT in the filter
    12	//     hook (the hook persists user-settable filter axes; the page
    13	//     index is a transient "where am I in this scroll" value that
    14	//     should NOT survive a reload). The page slice math runs through
    15	//     `applyPagination` which also clamps the index, so a stale
    16	//     `pageIndex` from a shrunk row set self-heals on the next render.
    17	//   - Mutation outcomes (rescan + import) flow through a TOAST QUEUE
    18	//     (`useToastQueue` + `<Toast>`) instead of the M3-era inline
    19	//     `lastReport` text. Errors render a Retry action that re-derives
    20	//     the click-time intersection at retry time (mirrors the M3
    21	//     handleImport pattern; the same fresh re-derivation guards the
    22	//     pagination-cross-page race).
    23	//   - The Rescan button carries a "last rescan from this browser X
    24	//     ago" caption (read from / written to
    25	//     `distill-portal:last-manual-rescan:v1` per spec §Action Bar
    26	//     and Mutation UX). The caption is explicitly scoped to "this
    27	//     browser" because the backend runs its own scans the browser
    28	//     cannot observe.
    29	//
    30	// Selection model unchanged from M3: `selected: Set<string>` holds
    31	// backend `session_key` values; the `+K hidden by filters` caption
    32	// surfaces the raw-minus-effective gap; the click-time intersection
    33	// in `handleImport` continues to filter by the current FILTER
    34	// window (NOT the page window) so cross-page selection accumulated
    35	// through "Select all importable in current filter" still ships.
    36	//
    37	// The toast queue is rendered as a sibling of `<main>` for DOM
    38	// placement (the queue is a fixed-position overlay anchored bottom-
    39	// right). Note this sibling placement does NOT isolate React renders
    40	// — `useToastQueue` is App-level state, so push/dismiss re-renders
    41	// the entire App subtree. The reason the table render stays cheap is
    42	// memoization (`mergedRows` / `filteredRows` / `pageRows` are
    43	// `useMemo`d, so the heavy filter/sort/paginate chain doesn't re-run
    44	// on a toast push).

   102	  // mounted regardless of mode; CSS-attribute selectors on
   103	  // `<main>[data-narrow-mode="..."]` drive visibility. Default is
   104	  // "list" — on narrow viewports the user lands on the list and a
   105	  // row click promotes them to "session". On wide viewports
   106	  // (>= 900 px) the value is meaningless because both panes always
   107	  // render; we still keep state synced so resize → narrow doesn't
   108	  // surprise the user with a flipped mode.
   109	  const [narrowMode, setNarrowMode] =
   110	    useState<"list" | "session">(() =>
   111	      // If the URL pre-selected a row on mount, the user is
   112	      // expecting to see the right pane on first paint. Default
   113	      // narrowMode to "session" in that case so a narrow-viewport
   114	      // deep-link arrival shows the session pane immediately
   115	      // (matches the M1a design.md §5.3 / spec lines 597–611).
   116	      selectedRowKey !== null ? "session" : "list",
   117	    );
   118	
   119	  // M1a: deep-link pulse mechanism. If the URL pre-selected a row
   120	  // on initial mount, write the same row key into a separate slice
   121	  // so the matched list row applies `data-deep-link="true"` for one
   122	  // paint cycle. The cleanup path is "whichever fires first":
   123	  // either the row's `onAnimationEnd` clears it, or a 2 s safety
   124	  // timer clears it unconditionally (the row may never render in
   125	  // the session_not_found case). Click-driven selection NEVER fires
   126	  // the pulse (spec line 585) — that's why the pulse target is
   127	  // captured ONCE on initial mount, not whenever selectedRowKey
   128	  // changes.
   129	  const [
   130	    pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey,
   131	    setPendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey,
   132	  ] = useState<string | null>(() => selectedRowKey);
   133	  // Use a ref to the safety-timer ID so we can clear it from the
   134	  // animation-end path or from unmount.
   135	  const pulseTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
   136	  useEffect(() => {
   137	    // Only start the safety timer if there's a pulse target on
   138	    // initial mount. Subsequent state changes do not re-arm the
   139	    // pulse — the pulse mechanism is a one-shot per page load,
   140	    // matched against the URL on initial mount.
   141	    if (pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey === null) return;
   142	    pulseTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   143	      setPendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey(null);
   144	      pulseTimerRef.current = null;
   145	    }, 2000);
   146	    return () => {
   147	      if (pulseTimerRef.current !== null) {
   148	        clearTimeout(pulseTimerRef.current);
   149	        pulseTimerRef.current = null;
   150	      }
   151	    };
   152	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   153	  }, []); // mount-only; pulse target is captured at initial render
   154	  const onDeepLinkPulseEnd = useCallback((rowKey: string) => {
   155	    setPendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey((prev) =>
   156	      prev === rowKey ? null : prev,
   157	    );
   158	    if (pulseTimerRef.current !== null) {
   159	      clearTimeout(pulseTimerRef.current);
   160	      pulseTimerRef.current = null;
   161	    }
   162	  }, []);
   163	
   164	  // useToastQueue is App-level state; push/dismiss re-renders App's
   165	  // JSX walk, but the merged/filtered/sorted/paginated rows are
   166	  // memoized via useMemo (see `mergedRows`, `filteredRows`,
   167	  // `pageRows` below) so the heavy computation does not re-run.
   168	  // The toast UI is a sibling of <main> for DOM placement (it's a
   169	  // fixed-position overlay anchored bottom-right), not for React
   170	  // render isolation — push/dismiss still walks the App subtree;
   171	  // memoization is the perf gate, not sibling placement.
   172	  const { toasts, pushToast, dismissToast } = useToastQueue();
   173	
   174	  // "Last rescan from this browser" timestamp. Read on mount;
   175	  // written by the rescan success path (NOT on error). Held in
   176	  // React state so the caption re-renders without an explicit
   177	  // refetch round trip.
   178	  const [lastRescanAt, setLastRescanAt] = useState<string | null>(() =>
   179	    readLastRescan(),
   180	  );
   181	
   182	  // `now` is captured at refetch time so the relative-time renderer in
   183	  // SessionsTable + the M5 last-rescan caption do not ticker-update
   184	  // between fetches. The spec requires "Relative time is computed
   185	  // against a single `now` captured at render time and refreshed on
   186	  // each refetch" — using state means refresh-on-refetch automatically
   187	  // triggers a re-render of both consumers.
   188	  const [now, setNow] = useState<string>(() => new Date().toISOString());
   189	
   190	  // Filter / sort / search state (persisted to localStorage).
   191	  const { filters, setFilter, resetAll, setImportableOnly } =
   192	    useSessionFilters();
   193	
   194	  // Active page index. NOT persisted — page index is a transient
   195	  // "where am I in this scroll" value that should reset to 0 on
   196	  // mount. Page SIZE is persisted (in useSessionFilters); only the
   197	  // index is in transient state here.
   198	  const [pageIndex, setPageIndex] = useState<number>(0);
   199	  // Track the previous pageSize so we can recompute pageIndex on a
   200	  // pageSize change without resetting (keeps the first visible row
   201	  // visible). `useRef` so we don't re-render on every change.
   202	  const prevPageSizeRef = useRef<PageSize>(filters.pageSize);
   203	
   204	  // Tracks the most recent refetch controller so a pending refetch can be
   205	  // cancelled when the component unmounts or a newer refetch kicks off.
   206	  const activeControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
   207	
   208	  const refetchAll = useCallback(async () => {
   209	    activeControllerRef.current?.abort();
   210	    const controller = new AbortController();
   211	    activeControllerRef.current = controller;
   212	    const { signal } = controller;
   213	
   214	    const [sourceResult, storedResult, errorsResult] = await Promise.allSettled([
   215	      listSourceSessions(signal),
   216	      listStoredSessions(signal),
   217	      listScanErrors(signal),
   218	    ]);
   219	    if (signal.aborted) {
   220	      return;
   221	    }
   222	    setSourceState(toPanelState(sourceResult));
   223	    setStoredState(toPanelState(storedResult));
   224	    setErrorsState(toPanelState(errorsResult));
   225	    // Re-pin `now` so the relative-time cells refresh against the just-
   226	    // landed data, not the stale value from the previous render.
   227	    setNow(new Date().toISOString());
   228	  }, []);
   229	
   230	  useEffect(() => {
   231	    void refetchAll();
   232	    return () => {
   233	      activeControllerRef.current?.abort();
   234	      activeControllerRef.current = null;
   235	    };
   236	  }, [refetchAll]);
   237	
   238	  // Reconcile `selected` against the current set of visible source sessions
   239	  // whenever a refetch lands. If a previously-selected row disappeared from
   240	  // the backend (e.g. after a rescan removed it), prune its key so the
   241	  // action-bar count and the import POST body stay in sync. The
   242	  // `changed ? next : prev` guard keeps the reference stable when nothing
   243	  // was pruned so downstream consumers don't re-render unnecessarily.
   244	  //
   245	  // Importability is INTENTIONALLY not checked here: a row that's still
   246	  // visible but no longer importable (e.g. just got ingested via a
   247	  // separate flow) should remain in the raw set so the user can see
   248	  // their stale selection in the count. The click-time intersection in
   249	  // `handleImport` is the authoritative POST-time filter.
   250	  //
   251	  // Filter-window membership is ALSO not pruned here. A filter mutation
   252	  // does not clear selection (per spec); rows that fall out of the
   253	  // current filter remain in the raw `selected` set and surface as
   254	  // "+K hidden by filters" in the action bar. The click-time
   255	  // intersection is again the authoritative POST-time filter.
   256	  useEffect(() => {
   257	    if (sourceState.kind !== "ok") return;
   258	    const visibleKeys = new Set(sourceState.data.map((s) => s.session_key));
   259	    setSelected((prev) => {
   260	      let changed = false;
   261	      const next = new Set<string>();
   262	      for (const k of prev) {
   263	        if (visibleKeys.has(k)) {
   264	          next.add(k);
   265	        } else {
   266	          changed = true;
   267	        }
   268	      }
   269	      return changed ? next : prev;
   270	    });
   271	  }, [sourceState]);
   272	
   273	  const handleToggle = useCallback((sessionKey: string) => {
   274	    setSelected((prev) => {
   275	      const next = new Set(prev);
   276	      if (next.has(sessionKey)) {
   277	        next.delete(sessionKey);
   278	      } else {
   279	        next.add(sessionKey);
   280	      }
   281	      return next;
   282	    });
   283	  }, []);
   284	
   285	  // Memoize the merge step so the three downstream consumers (filter
   286	  // pipeline, click-time importable set, action-bar visible-importable
   287	  // set) share one cache. This bundles the deferred M2 nit (per-render
   288	  // re-merge across three call sites). Depend on `sourceState` /
   289	  // `storedState` (the stable references), not on the inline
   290	  // `sourceData` / `storedData` derivations whose array references
   291	  // would change every render even when the underlying data did not.
   292	  const mergedRows = useMemo(() => {
   293	    const src = sourceState.kind === "ok" ? sourceState.data : [];
   294	    const stor = storedState.kind === "ok" ? storedState.data : [];
   295	    return mergeSessions(src, stor);
   296	  }, [sourceState, storedState]);
   297	
   298	  // Filter + sort pipeline. `applyFilters` and `applySort` are pure;
   299	  // they accept new state objects each call, so wrapping the chain in
   300	  // `useMemo` is the natural way to keep render cost flat across
   301	  // re-renders that don't touch the merged set or the filter state.
   302	  const filteredRows = useMemo(
   303	    () => applySort(applyFilters(mergedRows, filters), filters.sort),
   304	    [mergedRows, filters],
   305	  );
   306	
   307	  // Distinct project paths in the current MERGED set (not the filtered
   308	  // set) so the project autocomplete still surfaces every project even
   309	  // when another filter narrowed the view.
   310	  const projects = useMemo(
   311	    () => distinctProjectPaths(mergedRows),
   312	    [mergedRows],
   313	  );
   314	
   315	  // Pagination: derive the page slice + the clamped page index. The
   316	  // `applyPagination` helper guarantees pageIndex is in
   317	  // `[0, totalPages-1]` so a stale state value can't render an empty
   318	  // page when filteredRows is non-empty. The clamped value is
   319	  // synced back to React state via the effect below so subsequent
   320	  // renders agree on the same index.
   321	  const {
   322	    pageRows,
   323	    pageIndex: clampedPageIndex,
   324	  } = useMemo(
   325	    () => applyPagination(filteredRows, pageIndex, filters.pageSize),
   326	    [filteredRows, pageIndex, filters.pageSize],
   327	  );
   328	
   329	  // Sync the clamped pageIndex back to state if the helper had to
   330	  // adjust it (e.g. a filter mutation shrank the row set so the
   331	  // user's last pageIndex now lands past totalPages). The
   332	  // `clampedPageIndex !== pageIndex` guard prevents an infinite
   333	  // setState loop.
   334	  useEffect(() => {
   335	    if (clampedPageIndex !== pageIndex) {
   336	      setPageIndex(clampedPageIndex);
   337	    }
   338	  }, [clampedPageIndex, pageIndex]);
   339	
   340	  // Page-reset on filter change. Per spec §localStorage robustness:
   341	  // "Changing any filter or the sort resets the page to 1." We list
   342	  // every filter axis explicitly so a future addition has to opt in
   343	  // (and we don't accidentally reset on an unrelated render). We do
   344	  // NOT list `filters.pageSize` here — that change is handled by the
   345	  // recompute effect below so the first visible row stays visible.
   346	  useEffect(() => {
   347	    setPageIndex(0);
   348	  }, [
   349	    filters.tool,
   350	    filters.status,
   351	    filters.storage,
   352	    filters.importableOnly,
   353	    filters.project,
   354	    filters.search,
   355	    filters.sort.field,
   356	    filters.sort.direction,
   357	  ]);
   358	
   359	  // Page-size change recompute. Per spec §localStorage robustness:
   360	  // "Changing the page size recomputes the current page so the first

exec

     1	// Section-level wrapper for the unified inspection list.
     2	//
     3	// `SessionsView` owns:
     4	//   - rendering the SessionFilters control bar above the table
     5	//     (the bar wraps in a `<details>` disclosure below 1100 px per
     6	//     M1b; the wrap is internal to `SessionFilters.tsx`)
     7	//   - the per-section error banner when ONE side fetch failed
     8	//     (per-panel error isolation rule carried over from Phase 3 F1:
     9	//     a source 500 must not blank the stored-side rows, and vice
    10	//     versa)
    11	//   - dispatching to one of the four empty-state branches per
    12	//     `working/phase-4.md` §Filter, Sort, Search → Empty States:
    13	//       (1) "No sessions at all" — both fetches resolved to empty
    14	//       (2) "No matches after filter/search" — merged > 0 but
    15	//           filtered === 0 because of an active filter
    16	//       (3) "Nothing to import in the current filter" — filtered > 0
    17	//           but zero importable rows
    18	//       (4) "Partial fetch failure" — one of (source, stored) errored;
    19	//           the per-section banner from M2 still renders + the
    20	//           unified table shows the surviving rows
    21	//   - As of M1b the view also relocates `<ActionBar>` and
    22	//     `<Pagination>` into a single `.list-pane-footer` strip at the
    23	//     bottom of the list pane. The strip is `position: sticky;
    24	//     bottom: 0` so the footer is always visible regardless of
    25	//     scroll. Pagination renders ABOVE ActionBar inside the footer
    26	//     (per spec lines 555 + design.md §3.3).
    27	//
    28	// As of M2b the SessionsView NO LONGER OWNS the drawer state. The
    29	// Phase-4 modal `<Drawer>` + `<SessionDetail>` are unmounted from
    30	// the running app — selection now flows through the right-pane
    31	// `<SessionView>` with its four-tab shell (App.tsx → SessionView).
    32	// Drawer.tsx + SessionDetail.tsx remain on disk through M5 per
    33	// Resolved Decision #6; M6 deletes them.

    35	// `App.tsx` retains ownership of fetch state, the merged + filtered

    58	export type PanelState<T> =
    59	  | { kind: "loading" }
    60	  | { kind: "ok"; data: T }
    61	  | { kind: "error"; message: string };

    63	export type SessionsViewProps = {
    64	  sourceState: PanelState<SourceSessionView[]>;
    65	  storedState: PanelState<StoredSessionView[]>;
    66	  /** Merged but unfiltered rows. Used to distinguish "no sessions at all"
    67	   *  from "no matches after filter/search". */
    68	  mergedRows: SessionRow[];
    69	  /** Merged + filtered + sorted rows (full set across all pages). The

   126	  onDeepLinkPulseEnd?: (rowKey: string) => void;

   129	export function SessionsView({
   130	  sourceState,
   131	  storedState,
   132	  mergedRows,
   133	  filteredRows,
   134	  pageRows,
   135	  pageIndex,
   136	  pageSize,
   137	  onChangePage,
   138	  onChangePageSize,
   139	  filters,
   140	  projects,
   141	  setFilter,
   142	  setImportableOnly,
   143	  resetAll,
   144	  selected,
   145	  onToggle,
   146	  onToggleAll,
   147	  onRetry,
   148	  onRescan,
   149	  rescanPending,
   150	  pending,
   151	  selectedCount,
   152	  hiddenByFilterCount,
   153	  onImport,
   154	  onClearHidden,
   155	  onClearSelection,
   156	  lastRescanAt,
   157	  now,

   159	  onSelectRow,

   161	  onDeepLinkPulseEnd,
   162	}: SessionsViewProps) {
   163	  // Both sides still loading: render a single "loading" hint. This
   164	  // mirrors the Phase 3 PanelBody behavior so the user sees feedback
   165	  // during the initial fetch.
   166	  if (sourceState.kind === "loading" && storedState.kind === "loading") {
   167	    return <p>Loading sessions...</p>;
   168	  }
   169	
   170	  // Both sides errored: full-section "no sessions could be loaded"
   171	  // with a Retry. The other partial-fetch-failure branches (one side
   172	  // ok, one side errored) render the unified table built from the
   173	  // surviving side ONLY, plus a banner.
   174	  if (sourceState.kind === "error" && storedState.kind === "error") {
   175	    return (
   176	      <div className="empty">
   177	        <p role="alert">
   178	          No sessions could be loaded. Source error:{" "}
   179	          {sourceState.message}. Stored error: {storedState.message}.
   180	        </p>
   181	        <p>
   182	          <button type="button" onClick={onRetry}>
   183	            Retry
   184	          </button>
   185	        </p>
   186	      </div>
   187	    );
   188	  }
   189	
   190	  // Decide which empty-state branch (if any) fires for the current
   191	  // (mergedRows, filteredRows) tuple. The branches are mutually
   192	  // exclusive; falling through means we render the table.
   193	  //
   194	  // NOTE: empty-state math reads from `filteredRows`, not `pageRows`.
   195	  // A 100-row filter that pages to "page 2 with 0 items because the
   196	  // user's stale pageIndex landed past the last page" still has
   197	  // matching rows; the pagination clamp in App.tsx fixes the index
   198	  // before the next render lands.
   199	  let emptyState: EmptyStateKind | null = null;
   200	  if (mergedRows.length === 0) {
   201	    emptyState = "no_sessions_at_all";
   202	  } else if (filteredRows.length === 0) {
   203	    emptyState = "no_matches_after_filter";
   204	  } else if (filteredRows.every((r) => !isImportable(r))) {
   205	    emptyState = "nothing_to_import";
   206	  }
   207	
   208	  return (
   209	    <>
   210	      {sourceState.kind === "error" ? (
   211	        <p role="alert">
   212	          Failed to load source sessions: {sourceState.message}{" "}
   213	          <button type="button" onClick={onRetry}>
   214	            Retry
   215	          </button>
   216	        </p>
   217	      ) : null}
   218	      {storedState.kind === "error" ? (
   219	        <p role="alert">
   220	          Failed to load stored sessions: {storedState.message}{" "}
   221	          <button type="button" onClick={onRetry}>
   222	            Retry
   223	          </button>
   224	        </p>
   225	      ) : null}
   226	      <SessionFilters
   227	        filters={filters}
   228	        projects={projects}
   229	        setFilter={setFilter}
   230	        setImportableOnly={setImportableOnly}
   231	      />
   232	      {emptyState === "no_sessions_at_all" ? (
   233	        <div className="empty">
   234	          <p>No sessions have been discovered or stored yet.</p>
   235	          <p>
   236	            <button
   237	              type="button"
   238	              onClick={onRescan}
   239	              disabled={rescanPending}
   240	            >
   241	              {rescanPending ? "Rescanning..." : "Rescan"}
   242	            </button>
   243	          </p>
   244	        </div>
   245	      ) : null}
   246	      {emptyState === "no_matches_after_filter" ? (
   247	        <div className="empty">
   248	          <p>No sessions match the current filter.</p>
   249	          <p>
   250	            <button type="button" onClick={resetAll}>
   251	              Clear filters
   252	            </button>
   253	          </p>
   254	        </div>
   255	      ) : null}
   256	      {emptyState === "nothing_to_import" ? (
   257	        <div className="empty">
   258	          <p>Nothing to import in the current filter.</p>
   259	          <p>
   260	            <button
   261	              type="button"
   262	              onClick={() => setImportableOnly(true)}
   263	            >
   264	              Show importable only
   265	            </button>
   266	          </p>
   267	        </div>
   268	      ) : null}
   269	      {/* Render the table when filteredRows > 0, regardless of
   270	          whether the "Nothing to import" empty state is also up.
   271	          The table is informative (rows describe what the user CAN
   272	          see) and the empty-state copy + affordance complement it
   273	          rather than replace it. The two table-suppressing branches
   274	          ("no_sessions_at_all" and "no_matches_after_filter") only
   275	          fire when filteredRows.length === 0, so the table never
   276	          renders zero rows in those branches.
   277	
   278	          The table receives `pageRows` (NOT `filteredRows`) — the
   279	          slice math lives in `applyPagination` and is clamped per
   280	          render so a stale `pageIndex` from React state never
   281	          renders an empty page when filteredRows is non-empty. */}
   282	      {filteredRows.length > 0 ? (
   283	        <SessionsTable
   284	          rows={pageRows}
   285	          selected={selected}
   286	          onToggle={onToggle}
   287	          onToggleAll={onToggleAll}
   288	          now={now}
   289	          selectedRowKey={selectedRowKey ?? null}
   290	          onSelectRow={onSelectRow}
   291	          pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey={pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey ?? null}
   292	          onDeepLinkPulseEnd={onDeepLinkPulseEnd}
   293	        />
   294	      ) : null}
   295	      {/* M1b sticky list-pane footer. Pagination ABOVE ActionBar
   296	          (top-down: Table -> Pagination -> ActionBar — per spec
   297	          line 555 + design.md §3.3). The footer's `border-top`
   298	          hairline is the sole separator from the table content;
   299	          the inner ActionBar `border-top` is preserved as the
   300	          secondary separator between Pagination and the buttons
   301	          row (two stacked hairlines is intentional per the UI/UX
   302	          reviewer's notes). */}
   303	      <div className="list-pane-footer">
   304	        {filteredRows.length > 0 ? (
   305	          <Pagination
   306	            pageSize={pageSize}
   307	            pageIndex={pageIndex}
   308	            totalRows={filteredRows.length}
   309	            onChangePage={onChangePage}
   310	            onChangePageSize={onChangePageSize}
   311	          />
   312	        ) : null}
   313	        <ActionBar
   314	          selectedCount={selectedCount}
   315	          hiddenByFilterCount={hiddenByFilterCount}
   316	          pending={pending}
   317	          onRescan={onRescan}
   318	          onImport={onImport}
   319	          onClearHidden={onClearHidden}
   320	          onClearSelection={onClearSelection}
   321	          lastRescanAt={lastRescanAt}
   322	          now={now}
   323	        />
   324	      </div>
   325	    </>
   326	  );
   327	}
   328	
   329	type EmptyStateKind =
   330	  | "no_sessions_at_all"
   331	  | "no_matches_after_filter"
   332	  | "nothing_to_import";
