 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// Component tests for the Tabs primitive (M2b).
     2	//
     3	// Coverage:
     4	//   1. ARIA roles + linkage: tablist + 4 tabs + each tab carries
     5	//      role="tab", aria-controls, aria-selected, tabindex.
     6	//   2. Roving tabindex discipline: exactly one tab has tabindex="0"
     7	//      at any moment (the active one); the other three have
     8	//      tabindex="-1".
     9	//   3. Click on tab → activates it (onValueChange fires; aria-selected
    10	//      flips; focus moves to the clicked tab).
    11	//   4. Keyboard nav: ArrowLeft / ArrowRight cycle (with wrap from
    12	//      first → last and last → first); Home jumps to first; End
    13	//      jumps to last.
    14	//   5. Selection follows focus (automatic activation per WAI-ARIA
    15	//      APG): a single ArrowRight press changes the active tab AND
    16	//      moves focus.
    17	//   6. Reduced-motion smoke (CSS source contains the @media reduced-
    18	//      motion block from global.css; the indicator's transition is
    19	//      zeroed by that rule — covered indirectly because the rule
    20	//      lives in global.css; we only verify the indicator carries
    21	//      `transition: transform` so the global rule has something to
    22	//      zero).
    23	import { afterEach, expect, mock, test } from "bun:test";
    24	import { act, cleanup, render } from "@testing-library/react";
    25	import { useState } from "react";
    26	import { Tabs, type TabDescriptor } from "./Tabs";
    27	
    28	afterEach(() => {
    29	  cleanup();
    30	});
    31	
    32	type TabId = "transcript" | "skim" | "raw" | "metadata";
    33	
    34	const TABS: ReadonlyArray<TabDescriptor<TabId>> = [
    35	  { id: "transcript", label: "Transcript", panel: "transcript-panel" },
    36	  { id: "skim",       label: "Skim",       panel: "skim-panel" },
    37	  { id: "raw",        label: "Raw",        panel: "raw-panel" },
    38	  { id: "metadata",   label: "Metadata",   panel: "metadata-panel" },
    39	];
    40	
    41	function Harness({
    42	  initial = "metadata" as TabId,
    43	  onChange,
    44	}: {
    45	  initial?: TabId;
    46	  onChange?: (next: TabId) => void;
    47	}) {
    48	  const [value, setValue] = useState<TabId>(initial);
    49	  return (
    50	    <Tabs<TabId>
    51	      ariaLabel="Test tabs"
    52	      value={value}
    53	      onValueChange={(next) => {
    54	        setValue(next);
    55	        onChange?.(next);
    56	      }}
    57	      tabs={TABS}
    58	    />
    59	  );
    60	}
    61	
    62	test("Tabs: ARIA roles + linkage — tablist with 4 tabs each carrying role/aria-controls/aria-selected/tabindex", () => {
    63	  const { container } = render(<Harness />);
    64	  const tablist = container.querySelector('[role="tablist"]');
    65	  expect(tablist).not.toBeNull();
    66	  expect(tablist?.getAttribute("aria-label")).toBe("Test tabs");
    67	  const tabs = Array.from(container.querySelectorAll('[role="tab"]'));
    68	  expect(tabs.length).toBe(4);
    69	  // Each tab carries id=tab-<id>, aria-controls=panel-<id>,
    70	  // aria-selected, and tabindex.
    71	  const ids = tabs.map((t) => t.getAttribute("id"));
    72	  expect(ids).toEqual([
    73	    "tab-transcript",
    74	    "tab-skim",
    75	    "tab-raw",
    76	    "tab-metadata",
    77	  ]);
    78	  for (const tab of tabs) {
    79	    const id = tab.getAttribute("id") ?? "";
    80	    const tabId = id.replace(/^tab-/, "");
    81	    expect(tab.getAttribute("aria-controls")).toBe(`panel-${tabId}`);
    82	    expect(tab.getAttribute("aria-selected")).toMatch(/^(true|false)$/);
    83	    expect(tab.getAttribute("tabindex")).toMatch(/^(-1|0)$/);
    84	  }
    85	});
    86	
    87	test("Tabs: roving tabindex — exactly one tab has tabindex=0 at any moment (the active one)", () => {
    88	  const { container } = render(<Harness initial="metadata" />);
    89	  const tabs = Array.from(
    90	    container.querySelectorAll('[role="tab"]'),
    91	  ) as HTMLButtonElement[];
    92	  const tabindex0 = tabs.filter((t) => t.tabIndex === 0);
    93	  expect(tabindex0.length).toBe(1);
    94	  expect(tabindex0[0]?.id).toBe("tab-metadata");
    95	  // Click on Skim → only Skim has tabindex=0.
    96	  act(() => {
    97	    (tabs.find((t) => t.id === "tab-skim") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    98	  });
    99	  const tabsAfter = Array.from(
   100	    container.querySelectorAll('[role="tab"]'),
   101	  ) as HTMLButtonElement[];
   102	  const tabindex0After = tabsAfter.filter((t) => t.tabIndex === 0);
   103	  expect(tabindex0After.length).toBe(1);
   104	  expect(tabindex0After[0]?.id).toBe("tab-skim");
   105	});
   106	
   107	test("Tabs: click activates and moves focus", () => {
   108	  const onChange = mock((_next: TabId) => {});
   109	  const { container } = render(<Harness onChange={onChange} />);
   110	  const skimTab = container.querySelector("#tab-skim") as HTMLButtonElement;
   111	  act(() => {
   112	    skimTab.click();
   113	  });
   114	  expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
   115	  expect(onChange.mock.calls[0]?.[0]).toBe("skim");
   116	  // aria-selected flipped on Skim.
   117	  expect(skimTab.getAttribute("aria-selected")).toBe("true");
   118	  // The other tabs are aria-selected="false".
   119	  const others = Array.from(
   120	    container.querySelectorAll('[role="tab"]'),
   121	  ).filter((t) => t.id !== "tab-skim");
   122	  for (const t of others) {
   123	    expect(t.getAttribute("aria-selected")).toBe("false");
   124	  }
   125	});
   126	
   127	test("Tabs: ArrowRight cycles forward; wraps from last to first", () => {

   129	  const tablist = container.querySelector(
   130	    '[role="tablist"]',
   131	  ) as HTMLDivElement;

   227	  // Selection-follows-focus means Enter/Space on the active tab is
   228	  // redundant. The native `<button>` element handles activation
   229	  // synchronously on click; we don't intercept Enter/Space in the
   230	  // keydown handler, so neither key changes the active value.

   241	    );
   242	    tablist.dispatchEvent(
   243	      new window.KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: " ", bubbles: true }),
   244	    );
   245	  });
   246	  // No state change beyond the initial render.
   247	  expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(0);

    54	let originalClipboardDescriptor: PropertyDescriptor | undefined;
    55	function setNavigatorClipboard(
    56	  value: { writeText?: (s: string) => Promise<void> } | undefined,
    57	) {
    58	  Object.defineProperty(globalThis.navigator, "clipboard", {
    59	    configurable: true,
    60	    enumerable: true,
    61	    writable: true,
    62	    value,
    63	  });
    64	}

    68	    globalThis.navigator,
    69	    "clipboard",
    70	  );
    71	});
    72	
    73	afterEach(() => {
    74	  cleanup();
    75	  if (originalClipboardDescriptor !== undefined) {
    76	    Object.defineProperty(
    77	      globalThis.navigator,
    78	      "clipboard",
    79	      originalClipboardDescriptor,
    80	    );
    81	  } else {
    82	    setNavigatorClipboard(undefined);
    83	  }
    84	});

     1	// Component-level tests for the M2b Raw tab body.
     2	//
     3	// RawTab is byte-equivalent to the Phase-4 RawPreviewBlock (extracted
     4	// from SessionDetail.tsx) PLUS the M2b "Not yet imported" branch
     5	// when `row.storedSessionUid === null` (Resolved Decision #18).
     6	//
     7	// Coverage:
     8	//   1. Source-only row → "Not yet imported" copy renders without
     9	//      firing a fetch.
    10	//   2. Stored row → loading / success / error / non_2xx state matrix.
    11	//   3. streamSessionRaw is called with sessionUid + an AbortSignal.
    12	//   4. Byte-cap caption ("Stopped at byte cap — full payload not
    13	//      downloaded.").
    14	//   5. Line-cap caption ("Showing first N lines of the raw payload.").
    15	//   6. Neither-cap caption ("Showing first N lines (full payload below
    16	//      the caps).").
    17	//   7. Retry button bumps the attempt counter → re-runs the fetch.
    18	//   8. Non-JSON fallback line renders with the "(non-JSON line)" marker
    19	//      and the .text class.
    20	//   9. Pre-aborted signal handling: unmount mid-flight aborts the
    21	//      controller; no follow-on state mutation throws.
    22	//  10. Post-cap unmount is a clean no-op.
    23	import { afterEach, beforeEach, expect, mock, test } from "bun:test";
    24	import { act, cleanup, render, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
    25	import { RawTab } from "./RawTab";
    26	import { RAW_PREVIEW_BYTE_CAP } from "./rawPreview";
    27	import type { SessionRow } from "./types";
    28	
    29	const NOW = "2026-04-25T12:00:00Z";
    30	void NOW;
    31	
    32	function buildRow(overrides: Partial<SessionRow> = {}): SessionRow {
    33	  return {
    34	    rowKey: "claude_code:fixture-raw",
    35	    sourceSessionKey: "claude_code:fixture-raw",
    36	    tool: "claude_code",
    37	    sourceSessionId: "fixture-raw",
    38	    title: "Fixture title",
    39	    projectPath: "/projects/fixture",
    40	    sourcePath: "/srv/sessions/fixture-raw.jsonl",
    41	    sourcePathIsStale: false,
    42	    sourceFingerprint: "fp-raw",
    43	    createdAt: "2026-04-22T00:00:00Z",
    44	    sourceUpdatedAt: "2026-04-25T11:55:00Z",
    45	    ingestedAt: "2026-04-25T11:50:00Z",
    46	    storedSessionUid: "uid-fixture-raw",
    47	    storedRawRef: "raw/uid-fixture-raw.ndjson",
    48	    hasSubagentSidecars: false,
    49	    status: "up_to_date",
    50	    statusConflict: false,
    51	    presence: "both",
    52	    ...overrides,
    53	  };
    54	}

    56	const ENC = new TextEncoder();
    57	
    58	let savedActEnv: boolean | undefined;
    59	function suppressActWarnings(): void {
    60	  savedActEnv = (globalThis as unknown as {
    61	    IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT?: boolean;
    62	  }).IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT;
    63	  (globalThis as unknown as {
    64	    IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT: boolean;
    65	  }).IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT = false;
    66	}
    67	function restoreActWarnings(): void {
    68	  (globalThis as unknown as {
    69	    IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT: boolean | undefined;
    70	  }).IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT = savedActEnv;
    71	}
    72	
    73	function makeStreamResponse(
    74	  chunks: Uint8Array[],
    75	  options: { status?: number; statusText?: string } = {},
    76	): Response {
    77	  let cancelled = false;
    78	  const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
    79	    async start(controller) {
    80	      for (const chunk of chunks) {
    81	        if (cancelled) return;
    82	        controller.enqueue(chunk);
    83	      }
    84	      if (!cancelled) controller.close();
    85	    },
    86	    cancel() {
    87	      cancelled = true;
    88	    },
    89	  });
    90	  return new Response(stream, {
    91	    status: options.status ?? 200,
    92	    statusText: options.statusText,
    93	    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-ndjson" },
    94	  });
    95	}
    96	
    97	function installSuccessFetch(lines: string[]): ReturnType<typeof mock> {
    98	  const body = lines.map((l) => `${l}\n`).join("");
    99	  const fetchMock = mock(
   100	    async (_input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
   101	      if (init?.signal?.aborted) {
   102	        throw new DOMException("aborted", "AbortError");
   103	      }
   104	      return makeStreamResponse([ENC.encode(body)]);
   105	    },
   106	  );
   107	  globalThis.fetch =
   108	    fetchMock as unknown as typeof globalThis.fetch;
   109	  return fetchMock;
   110	}
   111	
   112	let originalFetch: typeof globalThis.fetch;
   113	beforeEach(() => {
   114	  originalFetch = globalThis.fetch;
   115	});
   116	afterEach(() => {
   117	  cleanup();
   118	  globalThis.fetch = originalFetch;
   119	});

   122	  const fetchMock = mock(async () => makeStreamResponse([]));
   123	  globalThis.fetch = fetchMock as unknown as typeof globalThis.fetch;
   124	  const row = buildRow({
   125	    storedSessionUid: null,
   126	    storedRawRef: null,
   127	    presence: "source_only",
   128	    status: "not_stored",
   129	    ingestedAt: null,
   130	  });

   132	  expect(container.querySelector(".raw-not-imported")).not.toBeNull();
   133	  expect(container.textContent).toContain(

   135	  );
   136	  // Fetch was NOT called.
   137	  expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(0);
   138	});

   237	test("RawTab: byte-cap caption ('Stopped at byte cap — full payload not downloaded.')", async () => {
   238	  suppressActWarnings();
   239	  try {
   240	    const chunkSize = RAW_PREVIEW_BYTE_CAP + 16_384;
   241	    const lineHeader = '{"large_field":"';
   242	    const lineFooter = '"}\n';
   243	    const firstLineBody = "A".repeat(1024);
   244	    const firstLine = lineHeader + firstLineBody + lineFooter;
   245	    const remaining = chunkSize - firstLine.length;
   246	    const payload = firstLine + "B".repeat(remaining);
   247	    const encoded = ENC.encode(payload);
   248	    expect(encoded.byteLength).toBeGreaterThan(RAW_PREVIEW_BYTE_CAP);
   249	    globalThis.fetch = mock(async () =>
   250	      makeStreamResponse([encoded]),
   251	    ) as unknown as typeof globalThis.fetch;
   252	    const row = buildRow({ storedSessionUid: "uid-byte-cap" });
   253	    const { container, unmount } = render(<RawTab row={row} />);
   254	    await waitFor(() => {
   255	      const caption = container.querySelector(".raw-caption");
   256	      expect(caption?.textContent).toContain("Stopped at byte cap");
   257	    });
   258	    const caption = container.querySelector(".raw-caption");
   259	    expect(caption?.textContent).toBe(
   260	      "Stopped at byte cap — full payload not downloaded.",
   261	    );
   262	    unmount();
   263	  } finally {
   264	    restoreActWarnings();
   265	  }
   266	});

   272	      if (fetchMock.mock.calls.length === 1) {
   273	        return new Response("internal server error oh no", {
   274	          status: 500,
   275	          statusText: "Internal Server Error",
   276	        });
   277	      }
   278	      return makeStreamResponse([ENC.encode('{"after":"retry"}\n')]);
   279	    });
   280	    globalThis.fetch = fetchMock as unknown as typeof globalThis.fetch;
   281	    const row = buildRow({ storedSessionUid: "uid-error" });

   286	    });

   290	      "button.raw-retry",
   291	    ) as HTMLButtonElement | null;

   295	    });

   299	    });

   358	    expect(lines[1]?.textContent).toContain("not json");
   359	    expect(lines[1]?.textContent).toContain("(non-JSON line)");
   360	    expect(lines[0]?.classList.contains("text")).toBe(false);
   361	    expect(lines[2]?.classList.contains("text")).toBe(false);
   362	    unmount();
   363	  } finally {
   364	    restoreActWarnings();
   365	  }

   368	test("RawTab: unmount mid-flight aborts the in-flight signal (no follow-on state mutation)", async () => {
   369	  suppressActWarnings();
   370	  try {
   371	    let capturedSignal: AbortSignal | undefined;
   372	    globalThis.fetch = mock(
   373	      async (_input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
   374	        const signal = init?.signal ?? undefined;
   375	        capturedSignal = signal === null ? undefined : signal;
   376	        return new Promise<Response>((_resolve, reject) => {
   377	          if (signal !== undefined && signal !== null) {
   378	            signal.addEventListener(
   379	              "abort",
   380	              () => {
   381	                reject(new DOMException("aborted", "AbortError"));
   382	              },
   383	              { once: true },
   384	            );
   385	          }
   386	        });
   387	      },
   388	    ) as unknown as typeof globalThis.fetch;
   389	    const row = buildRow({ storedSessionUid: "uid-abort" });
   390	    const { container, unmount } = render(<RawTab row={row} />);
   391	    expect(container.querySelector(".raw-loading")).not.toBeNull();
   392	    await waitFor(() => {
   393	      expect(capturedSignal).not.toBeUndefined();
   394	    });
   395	    expect(capturedSignal?.aborted).toBe(false);
   396	    unmount();
   397	    expect(capturedSignal?.aborted).toBe(true);
   398	    await Promise.resolve();
   399	    await Promise.resolve();
   400	  } finally {
   401	    restoreActWarnings();
   402	  }
   403	});
   404	
   405	test("RawTab: post-cap unmount is a clean no-op", async () => {
   406	  suppressActWarnings();
   407	  try {
   408	    installSuccessFetch(Array.from({ length: 25 }, (_, i) => `{"i":${i}}`));
   409	    const row = buildRow({ storedSessionUid: "uid-post-cap" });
   410	    const { container, unmount } = render(<RawTab row={row} />);
   411	    await waitFor(() => {
   412	      const caption = container.querySelector(".raw-caption");
   413	      expect(caption?.textContent).toContain("Showing first 20 lines");
   414	    });
   415	    expect(() => unmount()).not.toThrow();
   416	  } finally {
   417	    restoreActWarnings();
   418	  }
   419	});

     1	// Presentational table for the unified inspection list.
     2	//
     3	// Receives the merged + filtered + sorted `SessionRow[]` plus the
     4	// `selected` set + toggle handlers from the parent. Selection keys are
     5	// always backend-provided `sourceSessionKey` strings — never the
     6	// React-only `rowKey` fallback for `stored_only` rows. The table
     7	// renders a checkbox ONLY on importable rows
     8	// (`isImportable(row) === true`) so non-importable rows cannot enter
     9	// the import POST.
    10	//
    11	// Per `working/phase-4.md` §Action Bar and Mutation UX, the bulk-select
    12	// affordance toggles ALL importable rows in the current filter window.
    13	// Non-importable rows are invisible to the bulk action.
    14	//
    15	// Per Phase-5 spec §Compact list rows (lines 520–531) the table is
    16	// compressed to FIVE columns: Select + Title + Status + Project +
    17	// Updated. The Title cell carries a two-line stack: bold title + tool
    18	// badge inline (line 1); muted mono rowKey + optional `(refresh)`
    19	// marker (line 2). The dropped Phase-4 columns (Tool / Stored Copy /
    20	// Source Path) remain reachable via the still-mounted Phase-4
    21	// `<Drawer>` until M2 ships the Metadata tab.
    22	//
    23	// Per spec, a row whose `statusConflict` is true gets a small
    24	// "(refresh)" affordance inside the Title cell — telegraphing the
    25	// "fetched state changed during load — refresh" hint. M1b warmed
    26	// the marker color from `--color-text-muted` (Phase 4) to
    27	// `--color-warn`; the M2a fix-up round 2 reverted it back to
    28	// `--color-text-muted` (italic) because the oklch retint regressed
    29	// the warn-as-text pair vs `--color-surface` to 4.21:1 (fails AA
    30	// 4.5:1 normal text). See `working/phase-5/designs/m2a-tokens/wcag.md`
    31	// "Codex fix-up round 2 closure" and the M1b designer's documented
    32	// mitigation in `working/phase-5/designs/m1b-shell/colors.md` lines
    33	// 56-58. The hint copy is unchanged; only the visual treatment
    34	// stepped back from "warning sienna" to "muted gray italic".
    35	//
    36	// As of M3 the "Updated" cell renders a relative-time string against
    37	// a single `now` captured at refetch time in `App.tsx` (passed in as a
    38	// prop) so the page does not ticker-update. The full ISO timestamp
    39	// stays available via the `title=` hover hint for users who need the
    40	// absolute value.
    41	//
    42	// As of M1b (Phase 5) the row click semantic shifts: a click anywhere
    43	// on the row OR pressing Enter while the row is focused calls
    44	// `onSelectRow(row.rowKey)` ONLY. The Phase-4 `onOpenDetail(rowKey,
    45	// triggerEl)` call is REMOVED from the row click path — the drawer no
    46	// longer auto-mounts on row click. The new entry point is the
    47	// vestigial "Open detail" button rendered by `SessionView` in its
    48	// `ready-placeholder` state. The checkbox cell still stops
    49	// propagation so toggling selection never triggers `onSelectRow`
    50	// (a11y bug magnet preserved verbatim from Phase 4).
    51	//
    52	// As of M6 (Chunk G) the status pill is rendered inline (the dedicated
    53	// `StatusBadge` component was retired). The transform —
    54	// `variant = status.replace(/_/g, "-")` for the CSS class and
    55	// `label = status.replace(/_/g, " ")` for the visible text — is
    56	// preserved byte-for-byte at the call site so the DOM shape stays
    57	// `<span class="badge {variant}">{label}</span>`.
    58	//
    59	// CSS lives in the sibling `SessionsTable.css` (selectors
    60	// `.table-wrap`, table chrome, `.badge.*`, `.title-cell*`,
    61	// `.select-col`).
    62	// Global utility classes (`.muted`, `.mono`, `.stack`, `.empty`) live
    63	// in `styles/global.css`; the filter-bar CSS is in
    64	// `SessionFilters.css`.
    65	import { relativeTimeFrom } from "./relativeTime";
    66	import { isImportable, type SessionRow } from "./types";
    67	import "./SessionsTable.css";
    68	
    69	export type SessionsTableProps = {
    70	  rows: SessionRow[];
    71	  /** Backend-provided `sourceSessionKey` values currently selected. */
    72	  selected: Set<string>;
    73	  /** Toggle a single importable row by its `sourceSessionKey`. */
    74	  onToggle: (sourceSessionKey: string) => void;
    75	  /** Toggle all importable rows: if any importable row is unchecked, select all; otherwise clear. */
    76	  onToggleAll: () => void;
    77	  /** Pinned-`now` ISO string used by the relative-time cell renderer. */
    78	  now: string;
    79	  /** M1a: rowKey of the currently URL-selected session. The matched
    80	   *  row carries `aria-current="true"` for assistive tech and the
    81	   *  selected-row visual treatment. `null` means no selection. */
    82	  selectedRowKey?: string | null;
    83	  /** M1a/M1b: setter for the URL-synced selection. Called on row
    84	   *  click (NOT on checkbox-cell click — the importability rule's
    85	   *  stopPropagation guard remains load-bearing). M1b removed the
    86	   *  Phase-4 `onOpenDetail` call from the row click path; the
    87	   *  vestigial "Open detail" button in SessionView is the new drawer
    88	   *  entry point (see `working/phase-5/designs/m1b-shell/design.md`
    89	   *  §3.5). */
    90	  onSelectRow?: (rowKey: string) => void;
    91	  /** M1a: rowKey of the row that should fire the deep-link pulse on
    92	   *  the current paint. Set on initial mount only when
    93	   *  `URLSearchParams.get("session")` was non-null; cleared by the
    94	   *  parent via `onAnimationEnd` OR a 2 s safety timer. Click-driven
    95	   *  selection MUST NOT set this — the pulse marks URL-driven
    96	   *  arrivals only (spec line 585 + Resolved Decision #19). */
    97	  pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey?: string | null;
    98	  /** M1a: notify the parent that the deep-link pulse animation has
    99	   *  ended on `rowKey` so the parent can clear
   100	   *  `pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey`. Optional. */
   101	  onDeepLinkPulseEnd?: (rowKey: string) => void;
   102	};
   103	
   104	export function SessionsTable({
   105	  rows,
   106	  selected,
   107	  onToggle,
   108	  onToggleAll,
   109	  now,
   110	  selectedRowKey = null,
   111	  onSelectRow,
   112	  pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey = null,
   113	  onDeepLinkPulseEnd,
   114	}: SessionsTableProps) {
   115	  if (rows.length === 0) {
   116	    return (
   117	      <div className="empty">
   118	        No sessions have been discovered or stored yet.
   119	      </div>
   120	    );
   121	  }
   122	  // Header bulk-select state is computed from the importable subset
   123	  // only. A row that cannot be selected (no checkbox) does not
   124	  // participate in "all selected" / "indeterminate" math.
   125	  const importableKeys: string[] = [];
   126	  for (const row of rows) {
   127	    if (isImportable(row) && row.sourceSessionKey !== null) {
   128	      importableKeys.push(row.sourceSessionKey);
   129	    }
   130	  }
   131	  const importableSelectedCount = importableKeys.reduce(
   132	    (acc, k) => (selected.has(k) ? acc + 1 : acc),
   133	    0,
   134	  );
   135	  const allChecked =
   136	    importableKeys.length > 0 &&
   137	    importableSelectedCount === importableKeys.length;
   138	  const someChecked =
   139	    importableSelectedCount > 0 &&
   140	    importableSelectedCount < importableKeys.length;
   141	  // When zero rows are importable we still render the header checkbox
   142	  // but it stays disabled — there's nothing to bulk-select.
   143	  const headerCheckboxDisabled = importableKeys.length === 0;
   144	
   145	  return (
   146	    <div className="table-wrap">
   147	      <table>
   148	        <thead>
   149	          <tr>
   150	            <th className="select-col">
   151	              <input
   152	                type="checkbox"
   153	                aria-label="Select all importable sessions"
   154	                checked={allChecked}
   155	                disabled={headerCheckboxDisabled}
   156	                ref={(el) => {
   157	                  if (el) el.indeterminate = someChecked;
   158	                }}
   159	                onChange={onToggleAll}
   160	              />
   161	            </th>
   162	            <th>Title</th>
   163	            <th>Status</th>
   164	            <th>Project</th>
   165	            <th>Updated</th>
   166	          </tr>
   167	        </thead>
   168	        <tbody>
   169	          {rows.map((row) => {
   170	            const importable = isImportable(row);
   171	            const checked =
   172	              row.sourceSessionKey !== null &&
   173	              selected.has(row.sourceSessionKey);
   174	            const handleRowOpen = () => {
   175	              // M1b: row "open" gesture is now URL-synced selection
   176	              // ONLY. The Phase-4 `onOpenDetail` drawer auto-mount has
   177	              // been retired — the vestigial "Open detail" button in
   178	              // `SessionView` is the new drawer entry point.
   179	              if (onSelectRow) onSelectRow(row.rowKey);
   180	            };
   181	            const isSelected = selectedRowKey === row.rowKey;
   182	            const isPulseTarget =
   183	              pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey === row.rowKey;
   184	            return (
   185	              <tr
   186	                key={row.rowKey}
   187	                tabIndex={0}
   188	                aria-current={isSelected ? "true" : undefined}
   189	                data-deep-link={isPulseTarget ? "true" : undefined}
   190	                onAnimationEnd={
   191	                  isPulseTarget && onDeepLinkPulseEnd
   192	                    ? () => onDeepLinkPulseEnd(row.rowKey)
   193	                    : undefined
   194	                }
   195	                onClick={(event) => {
   196	                  // Walk up from the click target to see whether the
   197	                  // event came from inside the checkbox column. A
   198	                  // click on the checkbox cell (or anything inside
   199	                  // it) toggles selection only — it MUST NOT trigger
   200	                  // the row-open path (a11y bug magnet). The
   201	                  // `event.stopPropagation()` on the cell handles
   202	                  // most cases; this guard is the belt-and-braces
   203	                  // backup in case the propagation interception is
   204	                  // bypassed by an event-time bubble re-fire.
   205	                  const target = event.target as HTMLElement | null;
   206	                  if (target?.closest("td.select-col") !== null) return;
   207	                  handleRowOpen();
   208	                }}
   209	                onKeyDown={(event) => {
   210	                  if (event.key !== "Enter") return;
   211	                  // Same checkbox-cell guard as the click handler.
   212	                  const target = event.target as HTMLElement | null;
   213	                  if (target?.closest("td.select-col") !== null) return;
   214	                  // Prevent the default form-submission behaviour
   215	                  // (no form here, but Enter on a focused element
   216	                  // can still trigger native actions in some
   217	                  // contexts).
   218	                  event.preventDefault();
   219	                  handleRowOpen();
   220	                }}
   221	              >
   222	                <td
   223	                  className="select-col"
   224	                  // Stop click propagation BEFORE the row's onClick
   225	                  // sees it — toggling selection should never trigger
   226	                  // the row-open path.
   227	                  onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
   228	                >
   229	                  {importable && row.sourceSessionKey !== null ? (
   230	                    <input
   231	                      type="checkbox"
   232	                      aria-label={`Select ${row.sourceSessionKey}`}
   233	                      checked={checked}
   234	                      onChange={() => onToggle(row.sourceSessionKey!)}
   235	                    />
   236	                  ) : null}
   237	                </td>
   238	                <td>
   239	                  <div className="title-cell">
   240	                    <div className="title-cell-line1">
   241	                      <span className="title-cell-title">
   242	                        {row.title ?? "(untitled)"}
   243	                      </span>
   244	                      <span className="title-cell-tool">{row.tool}</span>
   245	                    </div>
   246	                    <div className="title-cell-line2">
   247	                      <span className="title-cell-rowkey">
   248	                        {row.rowKey}
   249	                      </span>
   250	                      {row.statusConflict ? (
   251	                        <span
   252	                          className="title-cell-refresh"
   253	                          title="Source and stored status disagreed during load — refresh to re-fetch."
   254	                        >
   255	                          (refresh)
   256	                        </span>
   257	                      ) : null}
   258	                    </div>
   259	                  </div>
   260	                </td>

exec
/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/SessionsTable.tsx | sed -n '260,360p'" in /home/huwei/ai_codings/distill-portal
 succeeded in 0ms:
   260	                </td>
   261	                <td>
   262	                  <span
   263	                    className={`badge ${row.status.replace(/_/g, "-")}`}
   264	                  >
   265	                    {row.status.replace(/_/g, " ")}
   266	                  </span>
   267	                </td>
   268	                <td
   269	                  className="project-cell"
   270	                  title={row.projectPath ?? undefined}
   271	                >
   272	                  {row.projectPath ?? "—"}
   273	                </td>
   274	                <td
   275	                  className="mono updated-cell"
   276	                  title={row.sourceUpdatedAt ?? undefined}
   277	                >
   278	                  {relativeTimeFrom(now, row.sourceUpdatedAt)}
   279	                </td>
   280	              </tr>
   281	            );
   282	          })}
   283	        </tbody>
   284	      </table>
   285	    </div>
   286	  );
   287	}

     1	# M2b — Tabs primitive + SessionView shell rewire + SessionMetadata + RawTab + vestigial drawer-trigger collapse
     2	
     3	Design artifact for **Phase 5 / Milestone 2 / Chunk b**.
     4	Spec frozen at `working/phase-5.md` @ `05467ad` on `main`.
     5	Implementation predecessors: M1a closed at `a59b3f6`; M1b closed at `e8d80c5`; M2a (token rollout + Fraunces + noise overlay) closed on `main` at `c1602e5` / log entry `e05a1ac`.
     6	Designer: UI/UX subagent dispatched 2026-05-07.
     7	
     8	This is a **reference artifact**. The developer subagent re-implements
     9	against the production stack (Bun + Vite + React + TS + per-component
    10	sibling CSS). Nothing in this folder ships to `apps/frontend/`; the
    11	prototype's hex-fallback literals do not contaminate the
    12	`apps/frontend/src/` audit count.
    13	
    14	---
    15	
    16	## 1. Chunk scope summary
    17	
    18	M2b is the **structural rewire** that replaces the Phase 4 modal drawer
    19	with the Phase 5 right-pane tab shell. Six new visible surfaces land
    20	inside the existing M1a `<article class="session-pane">`; one
    21	M1a-vestigial affordance (the "Open detail" trigger) is removed; the
    22	M1a outer SessionView gains a 200 ms page-turn fade; and a new
    23	accessible Tabs primitive lands at `apps/frontend/src/components/`.
    24	
    25	The 8 visible surfaces this chunk touches (numbered against the brief):
    26	
    27	1. **Tab strip** — `<div class="tabs" role="tablist">` with four
    28	   `<button role="tab">` children plus a single absolutely-positioned
    29	   `<span class="indicator">` that slides between active tabs over
    30	   `--motion-base var(--ease-standard)`. Order: **Transcript / Skim /
    31	   Raw / Metadata** left-to-right. Default active tab AT M2b =
    32	   **Metadata** per Resolved Decision #11.
    33	
    34	2. **Session-pane minimal header** — title (Fraunces italic at
    35	   `--text-xl`) + tool badge + status pill + conflict badge.
    36	   `Copy path`, header-side subagent badge, sourcePathIsStale hint,
    37	   "Open raw" anchor all defer to M4. Narrow viewports (< 900 px)
    38	   render an additional "← Back to list" quiet text-button row above.
    39	
    40	3. **Tab panels (active + inactive states)** — active panel renders
    41	   inside the M1a tab-shell scaffolding. Each panel's React subtree
    42	   is **STABLE across tab switches** (NOT remounted): the outer
    43	   `<div role="tabpanel" hidden={!isActive}>` toggles the `hidden`
    44	   attribute on activation, and a 120 ms opacity-only cross-fade-IN
    45	   is driven by **CSS `animation-name` re-trigger** (set
    46	   `animation: tab-fade-in 120ms var(--ease-out) both` when
    47	   `isActive=true`; set `animation: none` when `isActive=false`).
    48	   Inactive-but-visited panels carry the `hidden` HTML attribute
    49	   (display:none + accessibility-tree exclusion). Inactive-and-
    50	   unvisited panels are not rendered at all (lazy-on-first-activation
    51	   per Resolved Decision #12). On `selectedRowKey` change the
    52	   `visitedTabs` set resets to `new Set([defaultTab])` and the
    53	   unvisited panels drop their content entirely.
    54	
    55	   **Do NOT use a React `key={activeTab}` on the panel content
    56	   wrapper.** Remounting the panel content on every tab change would
    57	   abort RawTab's in-flight `consumeRawPreview` consumer and refire
    58	   the fetch, AND in M3+ would re-trigger `useParsedSession` for
    59	   Skim/Transcript — this directly violates Resolved Decision #12's
    60	   keep-mounted contract (spec lines 650–658). The cross-fade is
    61	   driven by CSS animation re-trigger (above), NOT by React key
    62	   remount. The page-turn fade at the OUTER `<SessionView
    63	   key={selectedRowKey}>` layer DOES still remount (because a
    64	   selection change SHOULD reset parser state); only the inner
    65	   tab-panel cross-fade is non-remounting.
    66	
    67	4. **Page-turn fade** — outer `<SessionView key={selectedRowKey}>`
    68	   fades opacity 0→1 + translates `4px → 0` over 200 ms `--ease-out`
    69	   on every selection change. Implemented as a CSS `@keyframes
    70	   session-page-turn` on the `.session-pane` element; the React key
    71	   remount fires the animation. This is signature detail #2 from
    72	   §Design Language; M2b is the chunk that lands it.
    73	
    74	5. **Metadata tab body** — `SessionMetadata.tsx` extracted from
    75	   `SessionDetail.tsx` lines 160–256 verbatim. Same 18 fields, same
    76	   labels, same `mono` class, same `<dt>` / `<dd>` layout. Plus the
    77	   subagent sidecar badge (NEW for M2b per Resolved Decision #8 —
    78	   header version defers to M4; Metadata version ships now), plus
    79	   the sourcePathIsStale label swap, plus the statusConflict-derived
    80	   "(disagreed during load)" muted note, plus the Copy path button,
    81	   plus the "Open raw" anchor (rendered only when
    82	   `row.storedSessionUid !== null`).
    83	
    84	6. **Raw tab body** — `RawTab.tsx` extracted from `SessionDetail.tsx`'s
    85	   `RawPreviewBlock`. Byte-equivalent to Phase 4. Same caption
    86	   strings, same 256 KB / 20-line caps, same Retry semantics, same
    87	   AbortController-on-unmount. Plus the "Not yet imported" branch
    88	   when `row.storedSessionUid === null` per Resolved Decision #18.
    89	
    90	7. **Skim tab placeholder** — `<SkimView>` not yet implemented; the
    91	   tab renders an editorial "Coming in Milestone 5" muted line
    92	   (small-font, italic Fraunces label + chrome-font subline). Subtle.
    93	   NOT a chunky empty-state illustration.
    94	
    95	8. **Transcript tab placeholder** — same treatment as #7 with
    96	   "Coming in Milestone 4".
    97	
    98	Out of M2b scope: full session header (Copy path, sourcePathIsStale
    99	hint, header-side subagent badge, "Open raw" header anchor — all M4);
   100	truncation banner / parser warnings banner (M4); SkimView block kinds
   101	(M5); Transcript per-kind rendering (M4).
   102	
   103	## 2. Design intent
   104	
   105	### 2.1 How M2b serves the Archive-room aesthetic
   106	
   107	M2a landed the **language** (Fraunces, oklch ramp, motion tokens, noise
   108	overlay). M2b is the chunk where the **right pane becomes a reading
   109	surface**. Three calibrations:
   110	
   111	- **The minimal header is editorial, not navigational.** No surface
   112	  chrome, no border-strong, no shadow. The session title sits in
   113	  italic Fraunces at `--text-xl` over the warm-paper surface; the
   114	  tool badge and status pill sit alongside as quiet annotations. The
   115	  tab strip's hairline `border-bottom` provides the visual anchor
   116	  below it; the header carries no border of its own. This is the
   117	  "hairline over shadow" principle (spec line 25) at the level of an
   118	  individual section header.
   119	
   120	- **The tab strip is text, not chrome.** Four labels, 12 px gaps, no
  1445	  under font-swap + i18n. The production Tabs primitive already
  1446	  needs a React effect for ARIA + tabindex management, so the
  1447	  measurement consolidates into the same effect.
  1448	
  1449	## 11. Conflicts with spec direction
  1450	
  1451	None. All M2b deliverables align with the spec's frozen text and
  1452	Resolved Decisions. The two non-trivial calls (subagent badge in
  1453	Metadata at M2b; full header surfaces deferred to M4) are
  1454	explicitly captured by Resolved Decision #8 and the brief itself
  1455	respectively.
  1456	
  1457	The cross-fade fade-IN-only resolution (§7.2) is a direct read of
  1458	spec lines 87 + 654 — there's no contradiction, just a precise
  1459	mechanism that the spec leaves open. Documented here so codex sees
  1460	the reasoning.
  1461	
  1462	The WCAG 2.5.5 AAA touch-target shortfall (§6.6) is below the AAA
  1463	bar but at the spec's mandated geometry. Documented here as
  1464	accepted-risk so codex sees the trade considered.

codex
There’s a second, more substantial blocker: the indicator transform writes `scaleX(${width}px)`. `scaleX()` takes a unitless number; the prototype’s executable code uses `scaleX(${tabRect.width})`. With the `px` unit, real CSS parsing rejects the transform, so the indicator can remain at its default invisible `scaleX(0)` instead of sliding/growing.

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/types.ts | sed -n '1,80p'" in /home/huwei/ai_codings/distill-portal
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// UI-local row type for the unified inspection list.
     2	//
     3	// `SessionRow` is the join of `SourceSessionView` ⊕ `StoredSessionView`
     4	// produced by `mergeSessions.ts`. The rest of the unified-list code
     5	// (table, action handlers) consumes only this type so it does not have
     6	// to branch on whichever side a row originated from.
     7	//
     8	// Identity rules (per `working/phase-4.md` §Data Model in the Browser):
     9	//   - **Import identity** is always `SourceSessionView.session_key` as
    10	//     returned by the backend (`${tool}:${source_session_id}` — single
    11	//     colon, produced by the Rust `source_key` helper). The UI never
    12	//     constructs or mutates this value. Rows with `presence === "stored_only"`
    13	//     have `sourceSessionKey === null` and are not selectable.
    14	//   - **React row identity** (`rowKey`) is the same `session_key` for
    15	//     source-backed rows; for `stored_only` rows we synthesize the
    16	//     fallback `stored:${session_uid}`. Row identity is used only for
    17	//     React keys and for tracking which rows the UI is talking about;
    18	//     the `stored:...` fallback never enters the import POST.
    19	import type { SessionSyncStatus, Tool } from "../../lib/contracts";
    20	
    21	export type Presence = "source_only" | "stored_only" | "both";
    22	
    23	export type SessionRow = {
    24	  /** React key. `${tool}:${source_session_id}` for source-backed rows; `stored:${session_uid}` for stored_only. */
    25	  rowKey: string;
    26	  /** Backend-provided `SourceSessionView.session_key` when present; null for stored_only. The only value that may enter the import POST. */
    27	  sourceSessionKey: string | null;
    28	  tool: Tool;
    29	  sourceSessionId: string;
    30	  title: string | null;
    31	  projectPath: string | null;
    32	  /** Always populated. Source-side `source_path` when discoverable; otherwise the last-known `StoredSessionView.source_path`. */
    33	  sourcePath: string;
    34	  /** True when `presence === "stored_only" && status === "source_missing"` — `sourcePath` is a last-known location, not currently discoverable. */
    35	  sourcePathIsStale: boolean;
    36	  sourceFingerprint: string;
    37	  createdAt: string | null;
    38	  sourceUpdatedAt: string | null;
    39	  ingestedAt: string | null;
    40	  storedSessionUid: string | null;
    41	  storedRawRef: string | null;
    42	  hasSubagentSidecars: boolean;
    43	  /** Authoritative status. When both sides report status, source-side wins; the stored-side disagreement is recorded in `statusConflict`. */
    44	  status: SessionSyncStatus;
    45	  /** True when both lists carried the session and reported different statuses (a rescan landed between the two GETs). */
    46	  statusConflict: boolean;
    47	  presence: Presence;
    48	};
    49	
    50	/**
    51	 * Single source of truth for the importability rule. A row is importable
    52	 * if it has a backend-provided `sourceSessionKey` (i.e. `presence` is
    53	 * `source_only` or `both`) AND its status is `not_stored` or `outdated`.
    54	 *
    55	 * Both `SessionsTable` (which decides whether to render a checkbox) and
    56	 * `App.tsx`'s `handleImport` (which derives the POST payload) consume
    57	 * this helper so the rendered selectability and the wire payload cannot
    58	 * drift apart.
    59	 */
    60	export function isImportable(row: SessionRow): boolean {
    61	  if (row.sourceSessionKey === null) return false;
    62	  return row.status === "not_stored" || row.status === "outdated";
    63	}

- **Verdict**: needs changes

- **Findings**:
  - BLOCKING — `apps/frontend/src/components/Tabs.tsx:99` — design.md §3.1 / checklist 9–11: `scaleX(${width}px)` is invalid CSS; `scaleX()` takes a unitless number. The prototype uses `scaleX(${tabRect.width})` at `prototype.html:1130`. Because `.indicator` defaults to `scaleX(0)` in `Tabs.css:94`, a rejected inline transform can leave the indicator invisible instead of sliding/growing.
  - BLOCKING — `apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/SessionView.css:240-243` — `working/phase-5.md:622`, design.md checklist 24, colors.md H09: the header conflict badge uses the error recipe, but the spec requires the warning recipe. Actual error recipe contrast passes at 6.60:1 light / 6.91:1 dark, but expected warning recipe is 5.43:1 light / 7.74:1 dark and is semantically required.
  - NIT — `apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/SessionsTable.tsx:20-21`, `42-48`, `83-88`, `174-178` — design.md checklist 52: comments still describe the drawer / vestigial “Open detail” button as reachable, contradicting the M2b collapse.

- **Missing Evidence**: none

- **Required Changes**:
  - Change the indicator transform to `scaleX(${width})` and add a regression assertion that would fail on `scaleX(...px)`.
  - Change `.session-conflict-badge` to use `var(--color-warn)` in all three `color-mix()` declarations, and update its comment/test coverage as needed.

- **Notes**:
  - I verified `bun run test`, `bun run build`, hex count 24, token count 83, no protected-path diffs, and no undefined CSS variables in the M2b CSS.
  - Object-keyed panels, keep-mounted RawTab behavior, tabIndex matrix, and DEAD-BUT-TESTED headers match the approved design.

- **Verdict**: needs changes

- **Findings**:
  - BLOCKING — `apps/frontend/src/components/Tabs.tsx:99` — design.md §3.1 / checklist 9–11: `scaleX(${width}px)` is invalid CSS; `scaleX()` takes a unitless number. The prototype uses `scaleX(${tabRect.width})` at `prototype.html:1130`. Because `.indicator` defaults to `scaleX(0)` in `Tabs.css:94`, a rejected inline transform can leave the indicator invisible instead of sliding/growing.
  - BLOCKING — `apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/SessionView.css:240-243` — `working/phase-5.md:622`, design.md checklist 24, colors.md H09: the header conflict badge uses the error recipe, but the spec requires the warning recipe. Actual error recipe contrast passes at 6.60:1 light / 6.91:1 dark, but expected warning recipe is 5.43:1 light / 7.74:1 dark and is semantically required.
  - NIT — `apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/SessionsTable.tsx:20-21`, `42-48`, `83-88`, `174-178` — design.md checklist 52: comments still describe the drawer / vestigial “Open detail” button as reachable, contradicting the M2b collapse.

- **Missing Evidence**: none

- **Required Changes**:
  - Change the indicator transform to `scaleX(${width})` and add a regression assertion that would fail on `scaleX(...px)`.
  - Change `.session-conflict-badge` to use `var(--color-warn)` in all three `color-mix()` declarations, and update its comment/test coverage as needed.

- **Notes**:
  - I verified `bun run test`, `bun run build`, hex count 24, token count 83, no protected-path diffs, and no undefined CSS variables in the M2b CSS.
  - Object-keyed panels, keep-mounted RawTab behavior, tabIndex matrix, and DEAD-BUT-TESTED headers match the approved design.
